Experiments show that even strong closed-source models struggle on GAVEL, while the supervised baseline yields consistent improvements across grounding and explanation metrics.

</details>


### [140] [Term-Centric Hierarchy Induction from Heterogeneous Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26963)
*Elena Senger,Yuri Campbell,Jan-Peter Bergmann,Rob van der Goot,Barbara Plank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以术语为中心的层级分类法归纳框架，通过自动术语提取将异构文档映射到共享表示空间，结合领域先验与数据驱动聚类构建可解释的层级结构。在百万级英德多源基准测试中，该方法在跨源一致性和层级质量上优于基于文本和摘要的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类法归纳方法依赖文档级表示，捕获的是整篇文档而非与知识组织相关的特定领域概念，这限制了其在异构数据源间的泛化能力。因此需要一种能够从大规模异构语料中提取领域概念并构建可解释层级结构的新方法。

Method: 提出一种以术语为中心的层级分类法归纳框架：（1）通过自动术语提取将来自不同来源的文档映射到共享表示空间，实现跨源鲁棒对齐；（2）基于术语表示，结合领域先验知识与数据驱动聚类，构建可解释的层级结构。

Result: 在包含超过一百万篇文档的英德多源新基准数据集上，该方法在跨源一致性和层级质量两个指标上均优于基于文本和基于摘要的基线方法。此外，通过德国区域创新分析的案例研究，展示了该方法在技术格局映射中的实际效用。

Conclusion: 以术语为中心的框架能够有效从异构大规模语料中归纳高质量层级分类法，兼顾跨源泛化能力和可解释性，在政策分析、创新监测和技术格局映射等实际应用中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Organizing knowledge from diverse text sources into interpretable hierarchies is crucial for tasks such as policy analysis, innovation monitoring, and exploratory domain mapping. Existing taxonomy induction methods typically rely on document-level representations that capture entire documents rather than the specific domain concepts relevant for knowledge organization, limiting their ability to generalize across heterogeneous sources. We propose a term-centric framework for inducing hierarchical taxonomies from heterogeneous corpora that scales to massive document collections. Our approach maps documents from diverse sources into a shared representation space using automatic term extraction, enabling robust cross-source alignment. Based on these representations, we construct interpretable hierarchies that integrate domain priors with datadriven clustering. Experiments on a novel English and German multi-source benchmark of over one million documents demonstrate that our method improves cross-source coherence and hierarchy quality over text- and summarybased baselines. A case study on German regional innovation analysis further demonstrates its practical utility for technology landscape mapping.

</details>


### [141] [RedVox: Safety and Fairness Gaps in Speech Models Across Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26968)
*Beatrice Savoldi,Sara Papi,Wafa Aissa,Matteo Negri,Luisa Bentivogli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RedVox是一个多语言语音安全与公平基准，覆盖五种语言，基于真实人声构建。研究发现现有语音模型在多语言和非英语场景下存在严重安全漏洞，且语音输入会放大风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音模型在多语言、自然条件下的安全性与公平性研究严重不足——仅有8%的语音模型发布文档包含多语言分析。现有基准多限于英语文本，缺乏真实语音、多语言覆盖和自然使用场景的评估。

Method: (1) 调研现有语音模型发布的安全报告实践；(2) 构建RedVox基准：收集五种语言（英、法、意、西、德）的真实人声，涵盖不安全和不公平的刻板印象请求；(3) 在该基准上评估8个前沿语音模型；(4) 对参与RedVox数据收集的人员进行问卷调查，记录语音数据采集中的隐私与伦理挑战。

Result: 8个前沿模型中，非对抗性条件下安全漏洞普遍存在；非英语语言中漏洞更严重；语音（spoken input）相比文本输入会放大安全风险。数据收集过程中暴露出参与者隐私保护等社会技术挑战。

Conclusion: 语音模型在多语言场景下的安全性被系统性忽视，亟需构建更具包容性的多语言语音安全基准，并关注语音数据采集中的隐私与伦理问题，推动自然场景下语音安全研究的成熟。

Abstract: Speech-capable models are increasingly deployed in real-world applications across languages. Yet their safety and fairness beyond English settings and under naturalistic conditions remain understudied. We survey safety reporting practices across state-of-the-art speech model releases, finding that only 8% document any multilingual analysis. To address this gap, we introduce RedVox, a multilingual safety and fairness benchmark for audio and speech built on real voices, covering unsafe and unfair stereotypical requests across five languages (English, French, Italian, Spanish, and German). Evaluating eight state-of-the-art models, we find that vulnerabilities persist even under non-adversarial conditions, worsen in non-English languages, and are amplified when the request comes from a spoken input. Finally, by surveying the participants who contributed to RedVox, we document the unique personal and privacy challenges of collecting speech data with human participants, pointing to broader sociotechnical challenges in naturalistic speech safety research.

</details>


### [142] [Auditing Framing-Sensitive Behavioral Instability in Large Language Models for Mental Health Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26982)
*Abla Bedoui,Ashley L. Greene,Mohammed Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究了在心理健康对话场景下，大语言模型面对语义相似但语境框架不同的提示时，其内部表征和行为响应的系统性变异，揭示了框架敏感性对AI可信度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康等敏感对话场景要求LLM具备行为稳定性与一致性。然而，语义相似的关切可通过不同语境框架呈现，可能引发模型不一致的响应。现有研究多停留在行为层面，对框架相关变异如何在模型内部表征中体现知之甚少。

Method: 采用受控匹配提示（controlled matched prompts），覆盖多种语境框架条件，在多个指令微调模型家族上进行实验。通过层级探测分析（layer-wise probing）解码行为关联信息，使用留出框架探针（held-out framing probes）结合强词汇基线检验，并通过激活引导实验（activation steering）验证框架相关表征方向对下游行为的因果调节作用。

Result: 框架会系统地改变模型的解释性响应倾向；行为关联信息在整个Transformer深度中均可解码，但解码强度因架构而异；留出框架探针在各架构上始终高于随机水平，即使面对强词汇基线；激活引导实验表明，框架相关表征方向可部分调控下游行为输出。

Conclusion: 对语境变异的鲁棒性应作为评估心理健康等敏感领域对话AI系统一致性与可信度的重要考量维度。框架敏感性可能挑战用户对系统行为的预期，并使AI可靠性评估复杂化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being integrated into mental health support tools and other psychologically sensitive conversational applications. In such settings, behavioral stability and consistency are important for trustworthy human-AI interaction. However, semantically similar concerns can be presented through different contextual framings, potentially eliciting different model responses. Such framing-sensitive variability may challenge user expectations regarding system behavior and complicate the assessment of AI reliability. While prior studies have primarily examined such effects at the behavioral level, less is known about how framing-related variation is reflected in the internal representations of aligned language models. In this work, we investigate these effects using controlled matched prompts spanning multiple contextual framing conditions across several instruction-tuned model families. Across architectures, framing systematically alters interpretive response tendencies. Layer-wise probing analyses show that behavior-associated information remains decodable throughout transformer depth, with architecture-dependent variation in decoding strength. Moreover, held-out framing probes remained consistently above chance across architectures despite strong lexical baselines. Activation steering experiments further suggest that framing-associated representational directions can partially modulate downstream behavioral outcomes. Finally, these findings indicate that robustness to contextual variation may represent an important consideration when evaluating the consistency and trustworthiness of conversational AI systems deployed in mental-health-oriented interactions.

</details>


### [143] [ReaORE: Reasoning-Guided Progressive Open Relation Extraction Empowered by Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26986)
*Xin Lin,Liang Zhang,Guoqi Ma,Hongyao Tu,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReaORE提出了一种从粗到细的关系推理框架，通过关系过滤和关系预测两个阶段，结合多维度推理与嵌入相似度，解决开放关系抽取中的泛化难题和易混淆关系区分问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前OpenRE方法存在两大缺陷：聚类方法无法生成关系标签且泛化能力差；LLM直接生成方法缺乏足够判别力，难以区分易混淆关系。需要一种既能泛化到未见关系、又能精确区分类似关系的新方法。

Method: 提出ReaORE框架，包含两个核心阶段：(1) 关系过滤：通过多维度推理理解关系和实例，生成初始关系集，并利用嵌入相似度进行补充和筛选，确保目标关系被包含；(2) 关系预测：在候选关系集上通过细粒度对比推理，精准预测目标关系，有效区分类似关系。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的OpenRE数据集上进行大量实验，ReaORE性能优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ReaORE通过渐进式推理策略，成功结合了关系过滤的召回能力和关系预测的精确判别能力，有效提升了开放关系抽取的泛化性能和区分度。

Abstract: Open Relation Extraction (OpenRE) requires a model to extract unseen relations between head and tail entities from unstructured text for real-world applications. The core challenge of OpenRE lies in achieving reliable generalization to unseen relation types. Current OpenRE approaches either employ clustering techniques, which cannot generate relation labels and suffer from poor generalization, or rely on direct relation label generation via Large Language Models (LLMs), which lack sufficient discriminative capacity to distinguish easily confused relations. To address these limitations, we propose Reasoning-guided progressive OpenRE (ReaORE), a framework for performing relation extraction through coarse-to-fine relation reasoning. Specifically, ReaORE consists of two key stages: (i) relation filtering, which reasons over multiple aspects to understand relations and instances, yielding an initial relation set, and further supplements and filters relations via embedding-based similarity to ensure the target relation is included; (ii) relation prediction, which aims to predict the target relations from the above set via fine-grained comparative reasoning to better distinguish easily confused relations. Extensive experiments on two widely used OpenRE datasets demonstrate that ReaORE outperforms existing baselines.

</details>


### [144] [Where Do Models Find Happiness? Emotion Vectors in Open-Source LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26987)
*Sinie van der Ben,Raphaël Baur,Yannick Metz,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509和Gemma-4-E4B-it两个开源模型中复现了Claude Sonnet 4.5的情绪向量发现，验证了情绪表征的泛化性，并揭示了效价(valence)表征在模型深度上涌现模式的显著差异，以及唤醒度(arousal)编码对提取语料的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Claude Sonnet 4.5中发现的情绪向量（emotion vectors）在开源模型中的泛化性，探究不同模型架构和深度下情绪表征（效价与唤醒度）的几何结构与涌现规律。

Method: 在Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509和Gemma-4-E4B-it两个开源模型的全部层中，使用两种模型生成的语料提取情绪对比向量（emotion contrast vectors），分析其与效价和唤醒度的PC1/PC2相关性及跨层变化模式。

Result: 两模型均恢复了效价几何结构，PC1-效价峰值相关系数分别为r=0.76和r=0.83，接近Claude的r=0.81。但效价表征的层深模式相反：Gemma早期层编码强、后期坍塌，Apertus早期缺失、中期涌现。唤醒度编码依赖提取语料：Gemma生成故事语料下PC2-唤醒度相关性更强（r达0.45），Apertus生成语料下较弱（r≤0.21）。

Conclusion: 情绪向量的效价几何结构在开源模型中具有较好的泛化性，但其在模型深度上的表征动力学因架构而异；唤醒度表征高度依赖语料中的线索分布，稳定性不及效价。已开源实验代码与数据集。

Abstract: Recent work identified emotion vectors in Claude Sonnet 4.5, which are internal representations that encode emotion concepts, causally influence behavior, and exhibit geometry mirroring human psychological structure. We test the generality of these findings in two open-weight models, Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509 and Gemma-4-E4B-it, extracting emotion contrast vectors across all layers, using two model-generated corpora. We recover valence geometry for both models, with peak PC1--valence correlations of $r = 0.76$ and $r = 0.83$, approaching the $r = 0.81$ reported for Claude.Beyond replication, we observe notable differences in how valence representations emerge across model depth. In Gemma-4-E4B-it, valence is strongly encoded in early layers but collapses towards later layers, whereas Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509 exhibits the opposite pattern, with valence representations absent in early layers, but emerging at mid depths. Arousal encoding, in contrast, is sensitive to the extraction corpus: both models show stronger PC2--arousal alignment with Gemma-generated stories ($r$ up to $0.45$) than Apertus-generated ones ($r \leq 0.21$), suggesting arousal-relevant cues are unevenly distributed across generated corpora. We open-source our experiment code and dataset for reproducible investigation of emotion representations across language model architectures.

</details>


### [145] [MinGram: A Minimalist Unigram Tokenizer with High Compression and Competitive Morphological Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27019)
*Sander Land*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MinGram 是一种极简化的 Unigram 分词器，它保留了 Unigram 的 token 列表表示，但通过 BPE 种子词汇表、最小 token 路径上的 Hard EM 以及单次扁平分数剪枝来大幅简化训练流程，在压缩率和下游任务质量上均优于 BPE 和标准 Unigram。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unigram 分词器虽然具有优雅的词汇表编辑能力，但其训练过程依赖后缀数组、前向-后向传播和迭代剪枝循环，计算复杂且沉重。作者希望保留 Unigram 表示优势的同时，将其训练简化到仅需分词器推理本身的轻量程度。

Method: 提出 MinGram，核心设计包括：(1) 使用 BPE 派生的种子词汇表代替从零初始化；(2) 采用最小 token 数路径上的 Hard EM 替代标准 Unigram 的软期望最大化；(3) 仅执行单次扁平分数剪枝，去掉迭代剪枝循环；(4) 以 token 数量作为主要优化目标，Unigram 分数仅用于平局决胜。

Result: 跨六种语言的实验表明：MinGram 的压缩效果优于 BPE 和标准 Unigram；其压缩导向变体可匹敌最强的 token 数量压缩方法，同时保持显著更高的形态对齐度；在下游语言模型训练中，Unigram 家族分词器（MinGram 位列最优）在 bits-per-byte 指标上一致超越 BPE。

Conclusion: MinGram 成功在 Unigram 框架内实现了极简训练，证明以 token 数量为主目标、概率分数为辅的策略能够在保持形态对齐和下游质量的同时，获得纯 token 计数方法的压缩优势，为分词器的轻量化训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: The Unigram tokenizer uses an elegant representation which makes it straightforward to edit vocabularies, but its training is comparatively heavy and complex. We introduce MinGram (Minimalist Unigram), which keeps the token-list representation but simplifies training using a BPE-derived seed vocabulary, Hard EM on a minimum-token path, and a single flat score-pruning step. This removes the suffix array, the forward-backward pass, and the iterative prune loop, leaving a procedure that requires little beyond tokenizer inference itself. By making token count the primary objective and using a Unigram score only as a tiebreak, MinGram keeps the compression of pure token-count methods while retaining much of the morphological alignment and downstream quality of probabilistic ones. Across six languages, MinGram compresses better than both BPE and standard Unigram, and a compression-oriented variant matches the strongest token-count compressors while retaining substantially higher morphological alignment. In controlled downstream language-model training, Unigram-family tokenizers, with MinGram among the best, consistently beat BPE in bits-per-byte.

</details>


### [146] [Improving General Role-Playing Agents via Psychology-Grounded Reasoning and Role-Aware Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27025)
*Zhenhua Xu,Dongsheng Chen,Jian Li,Yitong Lin,Zhebo Wang,Jiafu Wu,Yizhang Jin,Chengjie Wang,Meng Han,Yabiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Psy-CoT（心理学根基的思维链框架）和RAPO（角色感知策略优化），解决角色扮演代理在自然语言角色设定下缺乏深度内部推理、分布外泛化差以及强化学习中奖励黑客的问题。Psy-CoT将推理分解为交互感知、心理共情和逻辑构建三步；RAPO利用角色-词元互信息非对称加权梯度，放大角色特定词元信号。实验表明两者均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前监督微调范式使角色扮演模型仅模仿表面行为，缺乏类人的内部思维过程，导致分布外泛化能力差。此外，基于LLM奖励模型的强化学习中，通用短语和真正角色特定短语获得相同梯度信号，导致"奖励黑客"问题累积，误导模型选择。

Method: (1) Psy-CoT：提出基于心理学的思维链框架，将预回复推理分解为三步——交互感知（理解情境）、心理共情（从角色设定出发进行情感与动机推理）、逻辑构建（生成连贯回复）。(2) RAPO：角色感知策略优化，利用角色设定与生成词元之间的互信息对梯度进行非对称加权——在正优势下放大角色特定词元的梯度，在负优势下衰减之，从而抑制奖励黑客行为。

Result: 在CoSER、CharacterBench和CharacterEval三个基准上，Psy-CoT优于现有角色扮演思维链方法；RAPO在多个模型规模上均一致超越GRPO。

Conclusion: Psy-CoT通过结构化心理推理赋予模型动态"思考"角色设定的能力，RAPO通过互信息引导的非对称梯度加权有效解决LLM奖励模型下的奖励黑客问题。二者结合可构建更忠实、泛化能力更强的通用角色扮演代理。

Abstract: Building general-purpose role-playing agents that faithfully portray any character from a natural-language profile remains challenging. The dominant paradigm -- supervised fine-tuning -- encourages behavioral mimicry without deep, human-like internal thought processes, resulting in poor out-of-distribution generalization. Therefore, we propose \textbf{Psy-CoT}, a psychology-grounded chain-of-thought framework that decomposes pre-response reasoning into three role-specific steps -- \emph{Interaction Perception}, \emph{Psychological Empathy}, and \emph{Logical Construction} -- so that the model \emph{thinks dynamically} from the profile rather than merely mimicking surface patterns. While structured reasoning provides a foundation, it alone is insufficient; reinforcement learning is essential to further align the model with character fidelity. However, we observe that under LLM-based reward models, both generic phrases that hack the reward model and genuinely role-specific phrases receive identical gradient signals -- this hacking accumulates over training, misleading the model into treating both as equally optimal choices. To address this, we propose \textbf{Role-Aware Policy Optimization (RAPO)}, which uses profile--token mutual information to weight gradients asymmetrically -- amplifying role-specific tokens under positive advantage while attenuating them under negative advantage. Experiments on CoSER, CharacterBench, and CharacterEval demonstrate that Psy-CoT outperforms existing role-playing CoT methods, and RAPO consistently surpasses GRPO across multiple model scales.

</details>


### [147] [NuclearQAv2: A Structured Benchmark for Evaluating Domain-Science Competence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27047)
*Henry Shaowu Yuchi,Michal Kucer,Benjamin H. Sims,Selma Peterson,Emily Taylor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NuclearQAv2基准，用于评估大语言模型在核工程领域的知识能力。该基准包含约1240个问答对，覆盖布尔、数值和口头三种类型，采用专家出题、现有数据集和LLM辅助生成的混合构建流程。评估显示模型在事实性问题上表现良好，但定量推理和概念理解仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核工程等高度技术领域需要事实知识、定量推理和概念理解的综合能力，但缺乏系统化的LLM评估框架。现有基准难以全面衡量模型在技术领域的可靠性，亟需一个多维度、可扩展的评估基准。

Method: 提出NuclearQAv2基准，包含约1240个问答对，分为布尔型、数值型和口头型三类。构建采用混合流水线：结合专家编写的题目、已有数据集，以及利用结构化提示从领域技术语料中由LLM辅助生成题目。评估也采用结构化提示进行自动化响应评判，实现基准构建与评估的可扩展性。

Result: 在多种LLM上使用NuclearQAv2评估后发现，不同任务类型间性能差异显著。模型在事实性问题（布尔型）上整体表现良好，但在定量推理（数值型）和概念理解（口头型）上面临更大困难。

Conclusion: 多维度评估框架对衡量LLM在技术领域的能力至关重要。NuclearQAv2作为一个可扩展的基准，有效揭示了LLM在核工程领域的事实知识与深层推理能力之间的差距，为未来技术领域LLM评估提供了参考范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but ensuring their reliability in highly technical domains remains a significant challenge. In nuclear engineering, problem solving often requires not only factual knowledge but also quantitative reasoning and conceptual understanding. To address the need for systematic evaluation in this domain, we introduce NuclearQAv2, a benchmark for assessing LLMs on nuclear engineering knowledge. The benchmark comprises approximately 1,240 question-answer pairs spanning three categories: boolean, numeric, and verbal. NuclearQAv2 is constructed using a hybrid pipeline that combines expert-authored questions, existing datasets, and LLM-assisted generation from domain-specific technical corpora. By leveraging structured prompting for both automated question generation and response evaluation, the proposed framework enables scalable benchmark construction and evaluation. We evaluate a diverse set of LLMs using NuclearQAv2 and observe substantial performance differences across task types. While the models generally perform well on factual questions, quantitative reasoning and conceptual understanding remain considerably more challenging. These results highlight the importance of multi-faceted evaluation frameworks and establish NuclearQAv2 as a scalable benchmark for assessing LLM capabilities in technical domains.

</details>


### [148] [Towards Explainable Adjudicative Variance: Quantifying Judicial Discretion via Gated Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27069)
*Stanisław Sójka,Felix Steffek,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种法官感知的门控多任务学习架构，通过在编码器中引入细粒度结果分类法作为结构化正则，并利用门控融合机制动态调节法官身份信息的依赖程度，在英国就业法庭判决预测任务上以更少参数超越了生成式监督微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律结果预测需区分客观案件事实与裁判语境：基于案情的裁决依赖事实证据，而技术性处置可能受法官裁量权影响。现有方法将法官身份和分类法仅作为提示词或自回归目标注入，两种上下文信号在单一自回归通道中只能弱组合，未能有效解耦这两类信息。

Method: 提出Judge-Aware Gated Multi-Task Learning架构：(1) 引入细粒度结果分类法监督编码器，实现结构化解耦正则；(2) 设计门控融合机制（Gated Fusion），动态调节对法官身份的依赖；(3) 以LoRA适配的Gemma-4编码器配合门控架构，对比基于Gemma-4 26B-A4B的监督微调基线（将法官身份和分类法作为提示词或输出目标注入）。

Result: 在13,937份英国就业法庭判决上，所提方法以少一个数量级的可训练参数超越生成式SFT基线，达到新的SOTA；性能提升集中在最模糊和最稀有的结果类别上。此外，学习到的法官嵌入和校准曲线可定位裁判语境驱动预测的具体案件，具备可解释性。

Conclusion: 在身份条件化的法律结果分类中，条件化接口的选择比模型规模更重要：可微分结构化组合比基于提示的大模型组合更准确、更参数高效。

Abstract: Legal outcome prediction must disentangle objective case facts from adjudicative context. Merit-based rulings rely on factual evidence while technical disposals may hinge on judicial discretion. We propose a Judge-Aware Gated Multi-Task Learning architecture that explicitly models this distinction. We introduce a fine-grained outcome taxonomy to supervise the encoder, enforcing a structural regularization that disentangles distinct semantic pathways. This granular legal curriculum enables our Gated Fusion mechanism to dynamically modulate reliance on judge identity. We evaluate our approach on 13,937 UK Employment Tribunal decisions. We benchmark our design against supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of a Gemma-4 26B-A4B backbone, in which judge identity and the taxonomy are injected as prompt tokens or autoregressive output targets. The two contextual signals compose only weakly when forced through a single autoregressive channel. In contrast, coupling a LoRA-adapted Gemma-4 encoder with our gated architecture defines a new state of the art on this benchmark while requiring an order of magnitude fewer trainable parameters than the generative SFT baselines, with gains concentrated on the most ambiguous and rarest outcome classes. Beyond accuracy, the architecture is interpretable; learned judge embeddings and calibration profiles localize the cases where adjudicative context drives the prediction. These results indicate that, for identity-conditioned classification of legal outcomes, the choice of conditioning interface dominates scale: differentiable structured composition yields more accurate, more parameter-efficient models than prompt-based composition over a substantially larger backbone.

</details>


### [149] [The Riddle Riddle: Testing Flexible Reasoning in Large Language Models and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27103)
*Bella Fascendini,Kathryn McGregor,Max D. Gupta,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"谜语式谜题"（riddle riddle）范式，通过将经典谜语改写为仅需字面理解即可回答的问题，检验大语言模型（LLM）究竟是进行灵活推理还是模式匹配。结果显示，LLM在真正谜语上准确率高达84.9%，但在谜语式谜题上仅50.7%；人类则相反（50.5% vs. 80.5%）。LLM的错误中90.8%源于不当使用创造性推理，表明其表现可能来自记忆检索而非灵活策略选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨LLM在认知任务上的优异表现究竟是源于训练数据的模式匹配，还是真正具备灵活推理能力。现有基准难以区分两者，需要设计能有效分离表面形式与内容推理的测试范式。

Method: 设计"谜语式谜题"范式：将流行谜语改写为表面上保留谜语结构、但答案仅需字面解释的问题。通过对比LLM和人类在"真正谜语"与"谜语式谜题"两类刺激上的表现差异，并结合错误类型分析（创造性推理误用 vs. 字面推理过度延伸），揭示推理策略的灵活性。实验涵盖9个最先进LLM和100名人类参与者。

Result: LLM在真正谜语上准确率（84.9%）远高于谜语式谜题（50.7%），人类则完全相反（真正谜语50.5%，谜语式谜题80.5%）。LLM在谜语式谜题上的错误中90.8%是因为不当使用了创造性推理策略，而人类在真正谜语上的错误中仅57.6%源于过度依赖字面推理。两组都会犯错，但LLM的推理错误比例显著更高。

Conclusion: LLM在真正谜语上的强劲表现可能反映的是记忆检索而非灵活的策略选择。若无专门设计的刺激来揭示这种对比，容易将LLM看似推理的输出误认为真正的推理能力。

Abstract: Humans flexibly adapt their reasoning strategies to the requirements of a given problem. Large language models (LLMs) have performed well on many cognitive tasks, however, it is unclear whether this accuracy is a result of pattern matching from training data or flexible reasoning. Here, we introduce a novel paradigm to test this question: the riddle riddle paradigm. Riddle riddles are word problems written to mimic popular riddles, but altered so their answers only require literal interpretations. Identifying correct answers requires looking past the structure of each question and flexibly apply different reasoning strategies based on the content. If LLMs respond to surface features, such as form, a riddle-like structure should cause models to use an inventive reasoning strategy even when a literal interpretation suffices. Alternatively, if LLMs reason based on content, they should flexibly switch strategies when appropriate. Across two experiments with nine state-of-the-art LLMs and 100 human participants, we show humans and LLMs fail on this paradigm in opposite directions. LLMs were far more accurate on genuine riddles than on riddle riddles (84.9% vs. 50.7%); whereas humans showed the reverse effect (50.5% vs. 80.5%). Error analysis shows that 90.8% of LLM errors on riddle riddles (the condition where they show diminished performance) were due to inappropriate use of inventive reasoning while only 57.6% of human errors on genuine riddles were due to overextending literal reasoning. Thus, while both groups make mistakes, reasoning mistakes are made more often by LLMs than by humans. Overall, LLMs' strong performance on genuine riddles may reflect memory retrieval rather than flexible strategy selection, and without stimuli designed to elicit this contrast, it becomes easy to conflate LLM-generated outputs that look like reasoning with genuine reasoning.

</details>


### [150] [Forecasting With LLMs: Improved Generalization Through Feature Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27199)
*Humzah Merchant,Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器分析LLM在预测任务中的内部状态，识别出时间感知特征和前瞻偏差特征，发现放大时间感知特征可显著减少预测中的前瞻偏差，同时保持通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM在预测任务中是依赖时间特异性知识还是可泛化模式，并通过特征干预来因果性地引导模型朝向更基于历史的推理方式。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（sparse autoencoders）对LLM在多种预测任务中的内部表征进行解构分析，识别时间感知和前瞻偏差相关特征；随后在不同领域任务上对这些特征进行因果干预（放大/引导），观察对预测偏差和推理性能的影响。

Result: 放大时间感知特征能显著降低预测任务中的前瞻偏差（look-ahead bias），同时保持通用推理性能；而对候选前瞻偏差特征进行引导则未产生显著效果。

Conclusion: 可解释的时间特征可用于因果性地引导LLM转向更符合历史事实的推理方式，这为提升LLM预测的可靠性和历史一致性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Successful forecasting involves identifying patterns between historical and future states of the world which generalize to future observations. We apply LLMs to a variety of forecasting tasks and inspect their internal states using sparse autoencoders to understand whether they appear to rely on time-specific pieces of knowledge versus generalizable patterns. Our analyses identify features associated with both time-aware reasoning and look-ahead-biased reasoning. We then apply the LLMs to an entirely different domain and intervene on these features. We find that amplifying time-awareness features substantially reduces look-ahead bias on forecasting prompts while preserving general reasoning performance. In contrast, steering the candidate look-ahead-bias features does not produce an effect. These results suggest that interpretable temporal features can be used to causally shift LLMs toward more historically grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [151] [Syntactic Belief Update as the Driver of Garden Path Processing Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27206)
*Alan Zhou,Miloš Stanojević,John T. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"句法信念更新"（Syntactic Belief Update）指标，用广义Rényi散度衡量读者在遇到关键消歧词时句法信念分布的更新幅度，以解释花园路径句的加工难度——这一指标完全独立于词汇概率，比传统词汇惊奇度（lexical surprisal）更好地拟合人类阅读时间数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇惊奇度（lexical surprisal）通常能较好预测句子加工难度，但在花园路径句上表现不佳。花园路径句的核心困难在于句法层面的错误预期与修正，而非词汇层面的低概率，因此需要一个纯句法、非词汇的度量来捕捉这种加工困难。

Method: 提出一种主动预测机制：处理器在每个词后维持一个句法树上的概率分布（即句法信念），并在每个新词出现后更新该分布。当遇到花园路径句的关键消歧词时，句法信念会发生大幅更新。采用广义Rényi散度来量化这一更新幅度，该度量依赖于句法结构的分布变化，但完全独立于词汇项的概率。

Result: 所提出的句法信念更新指标在花园路径句的人类阅读时间数据上，比传统的词汇惊奇度提供了更好的拟合效果。

Conclusion: 该研究为心理语言学开辟了一个新的方向：探索纯粹非词汇的、基于句法信念更新的替代指标来解释句子加工难度，而非仅依赖词汇惊奇度。

Abstract: Garden path sentences present a processing difficulty for humans -- the sentence prefix leads the listener towards one interpretation, until the listener hears a critical word that shows that the initial interpretation was wrong. Lexical surprisal, a measure that usually predicts sentence processing difficulty quite well, fails to provide good predictions for garden path sentences.
  We propose an alternative that actively predicts a probability distribution over syntactic trees (its syntactic belief) and updates that distribution after each new word. If a processor is led down a garden path, syntactic beliefs will be wrong and will require a large update at the critical word. The magnitude of the update is measured with a generalized Rényi divergence. Crucially, this metric is dependent on lexical items, but is fully independent of the probability of lexical items. This Syntactic Belief Update provides a better fit to the human reading time data on garden path sentences. This suggests a new research direction examining purely non-lexical alternatives to surprisal for psycholinguistics.

</details>


### [152] [Paved with True Intents: Intent-Aware Training Improves LLM Safety Classification Across Training Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27210)
*Jeremias Ferrao,Niclas Müller-Hof,Iustin Sîrbu,Traian Rebedea,Yftah Ziser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出安全分类器应将用户意图作为提示与最终标签之间的显式信号进行建模。为此，作者引入了一个包含1724条困难安全提示的人类标注数据集AIMS，每条数据配有意图描述和危害标签。通过在监督微调、偏好学习、推理蒸馏和强化学习等多种训练范式下的实验，表明意图感知训练能显著提升安全分类器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分类器通常直接从提示映射到危害标签，忽视了用户意图这一关键中间信号。作者认为，明确建模用户意图可以提升安全分类器的鲁棒性和泛化能力，尤其是在处理具有歧义性或复杂语义的困难安全提示时。

Method: 1）构建AIMS数据集：收集1724条困难安全提示，人工标注意图描述和危害标签。2）在四种训练范式下评估意图感知训练：监督微调（SFT）、偏好学习（DPO）、推理蒸馏和基于GRPO的强化学习。其中DPO利用模型生成的意图错误进行优化，推理蒸馏以意图为条件，GRPO直接奖励意图忠实度。

Result: 1）DPO在模型生成的意图错误上优化，优于SFT；2）意图条件蒸馏在多数师生配对中优于仅推理蒸馏；3）GRPO直接奖励意图忠实度，在五个外部安全基准上取得最强平均性能；4）意图感知模型构成推理延迟-F1的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 忠实的意图建模是一种紧凑且高质量的监督信号，能够训练出更鲁棒的安全分类器。意图感知方法在性能与效率之间实现了最优权衡。

Abstract: We argue that safety classifiers should model user intent as an explicit signal between the prompt and the final label. To study this, we introduce AIMS, a human-annotated dataset of 1,724 difficult safety prompts, each paired with an intent description and harm label. We use AIMS to evaluate intent-aware training across supervised fine-tuning, preference learning, reasoning distillation, and reinforcement learning. Despite its size, AIMS enables competitive safety classifiers across training regimes: DPO from model-generated intent errors improves over SFT, and intent-conditioned distillation outperforms reasoning-only distillation in most teacher-student pairs. Most notably, directly rewarding intent faithfulness with GRPO yields the strongest average performance across five external safety benchmarks, while our intent-aware models form the inference latency-F1 Pareto frontier. These results show that faithful intent modeling is a compact, high-quality supervision signal for more robust safety classifiers.

</details>


### [153] [Compositionality and the lexicon in evolutionary semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27228)
*Fausto Carcassi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将形式语义学的组合性原则与进化建模相结合的框架，让词汇意义与组合函数在概念简洁性和交际准确性压力下共同演化。将该框架应用于量化语义演化，发现经典的"保守性"语义共相作为高效的系统级抽象自然涌现，并能调和量化词可学性经验证据与既有进化模型之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语义共相研究存在一个鸿沟：形式语义学强调句子意义通过词汇意义的递归组合产生，但大多语义共相的文献要么建模具有固定信号结构的词库，要么建模整体性组合而缺乏可解释的词汇部件。作者旨在填补这一空白，将形式语义学的核心洞见——组合性——系统性地引入进化建模。

Method: 提出一个协同演化框架，允许词汇意义（lexical meanings）和组合函数（composition function）在概念简洁性（conceptual simplicity）和交际准确性（communicative accuracy）双重选择压力下共同演化。通过分析帕累托前沿（Pareto frontier），考察量化语义（quantificational meaning）演化中最优权衡解的涌现特性。

Result: 在帕累托前沿上，"保守性"（conservativity）这一最著名的语义共相作为高效的系统级抽象自然涌现。该框架对句法结构敏感，能够调和关于量化词可学性（quantifier learnability）的经验证据与先前进化模型之间的不一致。

Conclusion: 形式语义学中关于句子意义的图景可以与进化建模有效结合。该框架为研究三类现象提供了模板：(1)语法范畴内的全局压缩；(2)句法论元的语义专门化；(3)词汇意义与组合意义的协同演化。

Abstract: Formal semantics has shown that sentence meanings arise by recursively composing lexical meanings, yet much of the literature on semantic universals models either lexicons with fixed signal structures or holistic composition without interpretable lexical parts. We introduce a framework that integrates this fundamental insight of formal semantics in evolutionary modeling, by allowing lexical meanings and a composition function to co-evolve under pressures for conceptual simplicity and communicative accuracy. We apply this framework to the evolution of quantificational meaning. Analyzing the Pareto frontier, we find that the most well-known semantic universal, conservativity, emerges as an efficient system-wide abstraction. The account is sensitive to syntactic structure and helps reconcile tensions between empirical evidence on quantifier learnability and prior evolutionary models. More broadly, the results demonstrate that the picture of sentential meaning developed in formal semantics can be productively combined with evolutionary modeling. The framework offers a template for studying universals that involve global compression within a grammatical category, semantic specialization of syntactic arguments, and the co-evolution of lexical and compositional meaning.

</details>


### [154] [CARVE: Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency for Chunk-Parallel Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27229)
*Sayak Dutta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CARVE 提出基于键轴擦除的内容感知循环架构，通过复用循环输出张量作为擦除门的内容信号，并用标量写入门替代值轴投影，解决了 GDN-2 中记忆盲门控、值轴擦除参数浪费及 WY 形式求解器不兼容三个缺陷，在 1.3B 参数规模上全面超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SOTA 的 delta 规则架构（GDN-2）存在三个耦合缺陷：(1) 门控机制是"记忆盲"的——仅基于当前 token 决定擦除，不参考已存储内容；(2) 值轴擦除掩码在值投影尺度上浪费参数；(3) 数学上阻碍了使循环训练效率媲美 Transformer 的 WY 形式三角块求解器。这些问题限制了循环模型的记忆效率与训练可扩展性。

Method: CARVE 围绕"仅在键轴上擦除"这一核心原则设计：(1) 证明键轴擦除是 WY 形式求解器保持有效的充要条件；(2) 复用已写入 GPU 内存的循环输出张量作为擦除门的免费内容信号，实现内容感知门控；(3) 将每个头的逐值写入门投影替换为单个标量，大幅减少参数。初始化时 CARVE 与 GDN-2 比特等价，质量差异完全来自内容门的学习。

Result: 在 1.3B 参数、100B token 训练规模下：WikiText 困惑度 15.72（比 GDN-2 降低 0.18，4.5-sigma 显著效果）；在 9 个常识推理基准上领先所有循环基线；在所有 RULER 检索探针上达到 SOTA；仅增加 0.4% 吞吐开销，峰值内存降低 13%，参数减少 19%。同时提供 6 个形式化定理，涵盖记忆容量、Lyapunov 稳定性、梯度流、表达力分离、帕累托最优块大小及混合最优性。

Conclusion: CARVE 通过单一原则——键轴擦除——同时解决了 GDN-2 的三个耦合缺陷，在保持训练效率的同时显著提升了循环模型的语言建模与推理能力。该工作表明，内容感知的记忆擦除机制对循环架构的性能至关重要，且键轴擦除是维持高效块并行训练的必要条件。

Abstract: Recurrent models must forget in order to remember, yet the state of the art decides what to erase without consulting what is stored -- the gate sees only the arriving token, not the memory it is about to modify. This memory-blind gating is one of three coupled defects in the leading delta-rule architecture (GDN-2): the value-axis erase mask wastes parameters at the scale of the value projection, and -- as we prove -- mathematically prevents the WY-form triangular chunk solver that makes recurrent training competitive with Transformers.
  We introduce CARVE (Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency), which resolves all three problems through one principle: erase only on the key axis. This is provably necessary and sufficient for the WY-form solver to remain valid. Within it, CARVE reuses the recurrent output tensor -- already written to GPU memory -- as a free content signal for the erase gate, and replaces the per-value write-gate projection with a single scalar per head. At initialisation CARVE is bit-identical to GDN-2; any quality difference emerges from what the content gate learns.
  At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B tokens, CARVE achieves WikiText perplexity 15.72 (minus 0.18 vs. GDN-2, a 4.5-sigma effect), leads every recurrent baseline on nine common-sense reasoning benchmarks, and sets state of the art on every RULER retrieval probe -- at 0.4% throughput overhead, 13% lower peak memory, and 19% fewer parameters. Six formal theorems cover memory capacity, Lyapunov stability, gradient flow, expressivity separation, Pareto-optimal chunk size, and hybrid optimality.

</details>


### [155] [Bridging Talk and Thought: Understanding Dialogue Dynamics Across Collaborative Problem-Solving Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27233)
*Zhengyuan Liu,Stella Xin Yin,Min-Yen Kan,Nancy F. Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双层编码框架，用于分析协作问题解决中的对话动态，特别关注人机与多智能体协作场景。该框架整合了认知/非认知问题解决与元认知调节机制，并在九个多领域数据集上验证了其有效性和泛化能力，揭示了元认知调节是深层协作的关键区分因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话分析方法存在局限性，随着智能系统从被动工具演变为具有自主推理和战略合作能力的主动智能体，迫切需要一套能够系统捕捉协作问题解决中对话交互动态（尤其是人机协作与多智能体协作）的分析框架，以优化和评估此类协作伙伴关系。

Method: 提出一个概念框架，采用层次化双层编码方案：第一层编码认知性与非认知性问题解决行为，第二层编码元认知调节机制。通过该双层结构实现对社会性对话与任务导向交互的整合分析。在九个跨领域数据集上进行验证以测试其有效性和泛化能力。

Result: 框架在九个跨领域数据集上展现出良好的有效性和通用性。分析揭示了人类与智能体如何协调知识、技能和努力来解决复杂问题，尤其发现元认知调节（如规划、监控、评估等）是区分深层协作与表层协作的关键判别因素。

Conclusion: 所提出的双层编码框架能够有效弥补现有分析方法的不足，为协作问题解决中的对话分析提供了系统性工具。元认知调节机制在深层协作中扮演着至关重要的角色，这一发现对优化未来人机协作和多智能体系统的设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: We present a conceptual framework for analyzing dialogue in collaborative problem-solving contexts, with an emphasis on the emerging dynamics of human-AI and multi-agent collaboration. As intelligent systems become active agents capable of autonomous reasoning and strategic cooperation, understanding the dialogic interaction during collaborative problem solving is increasingly important for optimizing and evaluating such partnerships. Our framework addresses key limitations in current analytical approaches through a hierarchical two-layer coding scheme that integrates cognitive and non-cognitive problem solving with metacognitive regulatory mechanisms. We demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability across nine datasets spanning multiple domains, and provide insights into how humans and agents coordinate their knowledge, skills, and efforts to solve complex problems, showing in particular that metacognitive regulation can be an essential discriminator of deeper collaboration.

</details>


### [156] [LMs as Task-Specific Knowledge Bases: An Interpretability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27237)
*Amit Elhelo,Amir Globerson,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过行为和机制分析，发现语言模型对同一事实的编码方式因任务而异——不同任务下同一事实会激活不同的参数子集，挑战了将模型参数视为统一知识库的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识库的一个重要属性是不同查询应返回一致结果。作者试图探究语言模型是否满足这一属性，即同一事实在不同任务（如问答、填空、判断等）下是否由相同的参数机制支撑。

Method: 采用行为分析和参数定位实验相结合的方法。行为层面，观察同一事实在不同任务训练过程中的共现模式；机制层面，通过参数定位识别不同任务依赖的参数子集，并分析链式推理（chain-of-thought）所激活的任务特异性参数。

Result: 行为上，在某一任务上学到的事实常常无法同时在其他任务中涌现；机制上，同一事实在不同任务下依赖不同的参数子集；链式推理的部分有效性来源于激活了超出评估任务本身的、任务特异性的参数。

Conclusion: 模型"知道什么"与"如何被提问"在参数空间中紧密交织，动摇了将LM参数视为统一知识库的类比，这对事实知识的可靠性与可控性具有重要启示。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) capture large amounts of factual knowledge applicable to a wide range of tasks, motivating the view of their parameters as a knowledge base. An important property of knowledge bases is that different queries for the same fact return consistent results, drawing on a single source of truth. We investigate whether LMs satisfy this property through behavioral and mechanistic analyses. Our results suggest that they encode knowledge in a task-specific manner. Behaviorally, facts acquired on one task frequently fail to co-emerge on others during training. Parameter localization experiments suggest a mechanistic explanation, revealing distinct parameter subsets underlying different tasks for the same fact. Finally, we show that chain-of-thought reasoning draws part of its effectiveness from engaging task-specific parameters beyond those tied to the evaluation task. Our findings suggest that what the model knows and how it is asked are intertwined in parameter space, undermining the "knowledge base" analogy and carrying implications for the reliability and controllability of factual knowledge in LMs.

</details>


### [157] [How Surprising Is Historical Italian to Language Models? Tokenization Tax, Comprehension Tax, and a Simple Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27275)
*Maria Levchenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个诊断框架，将LLM处理历史语言时的"历史难度"分解为四个维度（分词成本、预测不确定性/惊奇度、语义鲁棒性、上下文敏感性），并在三个跨世纪数据集上验证。结果发现编码成本与语义理解存在解耦：尽管历史文本分词代价高且惊奇度飙升，但嵌入表示的语义相似性依然稳健（>0.85），且简单的时序上下文提示可降低约60%的历史惊奇度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将历史语言难度视为单一障碍的做法过于粗糙，混淆了拼写变体、语言距离和预训练暴露程度等多重因素。数字图书馆工作流中亟需一个精细化、可操作的诊断工具，以区分LLM在哪些层面真正失效、在哪些层面仍可安全使用。

Method: 提出四维诊断框架：(1)分词成本——衡量历史拼写导致的token数量膨胀；(2)预测不确定性（惊奇度）——以surprisal量化模型对历史文本的预测困难；(3)语义鲁棒性——通过嵌入相似度评估表征质量是否退化；(4)上下文敏感性——测试时序提示对降低惊奇度的效果。在三个数据集上验证：自建17世纪意大利语语料库(1610-1689)、19世纪《约婚夫妇》作为高曝光对照、18世纪俄语民用印刷书作为拼写压力测试。

Result: 俄语和早期现代意大利语均产生25-30%的分词成本膨胀；但预测难度显著分化：17世纪意大利语惊奇度为现代意大利语的2.4倍，学术散文达3.2倍，而俄语仅小幅增加。关键发现是，尽管预测困难，嵌入相似度在所有数据集上仍保持>0.85，表明模型能稳健地表征历史语义。此外，最小时序上下文提示可将历史惊奇度降低约60%。

Conclusion: 历史文本对LLM施加一致的"编码税"，但这并不必然损害语义表征能力。数字图书馆可安全地将LLM部署于语义检索任务，但在生成式应用中需谨慎适配。简单的时序提示提供了一种轻量级、模型无关的缓解策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly critical to digital library workflows, yet their ability to process historical language remains poorly understood. Historical difficulty is typically treated as a monolithic barrier, conflating orthographic variation, linguistic distance, and pretraining exposure. In this paper, we propose a diagnostic framework that decomposes this difficulty into four distinct dimensions: tokenization cost, predictive uncertainty (surprisal), semantic robustness, and context sensitivity.
  We evaluate this framework on three datasets spanning three centuries: (1) a newly curated corpus of 17th-century Italian texts (1610-1689) digitized from original page images; (2) canonical 19th-century Italian "I Promessi Sposi" serving as a high-exposure control; and (3) 18th-century Russian civil print books as a contrastive orthographic stress test.
  Our results reveal a distinct dissociation between encoding cost and comprehension. While Russian and early modern Italian incur comparable tokenization penalties (25-30% inflation), their predictive difficulty diverges sharply. 17th-century Italian is on average 2.4 times more surprising than its modern equivalent - with academic prose reaching 3.2 times - whereas Russian shows only a modest increase. But predictive uncertainty does not imply representational degradation: embedding similarity remains robust (> 0.85) across all datasets, confirming that models can represent historical meaning even when generation is unstable.
  Finally, we demonstrate that a minimal temporal context prompt reduces historical surprisal by approximately 60%, offering a simple, model-agnostic mitigation. These findings suggest that while historical text imposes a consistent encoding tax, digital libraries can safely deploy LLMs for semantic retrieval tasks, provided that generative applications are carefully adapted.

</details>


### [158] [Multilingual Reasoning Cascades Need More Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27306)
*Arnav Mazumder,Dengjia Zhang,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov,Niyati Bafna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究翻译级联（translation cascades）在多语言推理中的信息损失问题，提出一种无需训练的上下文感知翻译级联方法——在最终翻译阶段保留原始问题、英文翻译及推理轨迹作为上下文，在9个多语言基准、3个骨干模型和285种语言上取得显著提升，尤其对开放式生成任务效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的翻译级联推理方法将非英语查询翻译为英语→用英语推理→再翻译回原语言，这一流程在结构上存在信息损失：每个阶段都丢弃了后续阶段可能需要的上下文信息，如文化背景线索、语域和消歧信息。这促使作者探索如何以简单、无需训练的方式改善级联中的信息流。

Method: 提出上下文感知翻译级联（context-aware translation cascade）：在最终的翻译模块中，将三类信息作为额外上下文输入——原始语言问题、英文翻译后的问题、以及英语推理轨迹。该方法无需额外训练，仅通过改变信息传递方式实现增益。

Result: 在9个多语言基准（涵盖多种任务类型）、3个骨干模型、285种高/中/低资源语言上评估，在开放式生成任务中跨模型和资源条件均取得显著提升。消融实验表明，原始语言问题承载了大部分有益上下文。

Conclusion: 研究强调需要更好地设计机器翻译级联中的信息流以缓解错误传播，并提供了一条简单可操作的默认策略：将原始用户问题保留到流水线末端。

Abstract: Translation cascades for reasoning translate the query from another language to English, reason in English, and translate the answer back to the original language. This is a competitive approach to multilingual reasoning, but structurally lossy, since each stage discards information later stages may need, including cues for cultural grounding, register, and disambiguation. We examine the benefits of a simple and training-free intervention: a context-aware translation cascade, which additionally provides the original question, the English translated question, and the reasoning trace to the context of the final translation module. We evaluate gains across nine multilingual benchmarks including various task types, three backbone models, and 285 high-, mid-, and low-resource languages, and demonstrate strong gains for open-ended generation across models and resource regimes. We show that the original language question carries most of the beneficial context. Our study emphasizes the need to better design information flow in machine translation cascades for mitigating error propagation, and provides a simple and actionable default strategy: preserve the original user question until the end of the pipeline.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Surface Forms: A Comprehensive, Mechanism-Oriented Taxonomy of Indirect Linguistic Encoding for LLM-Based Coded Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27314)
*Hamid Reza Firoozfar,Mohammadsadegh Abolhasani,Reza Mousavi,Paul Jen-Hwa Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向机制的间接语言表达（ILE）分类法，将其作为LLM提示的一部分，在检测社交媒体中的编码语言（如algospeak、委婉语和对抗性混淆）方面优于现有分类法和无分类法基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体用户为避免审核和监控，频繁使用间接语言表达来掩盖敏感含义。现有分类法多基于交际意图，缺乏对底层编码机制的抽象，限制了其对新兴编码语言的泛化检测能力。

Method: 提出一种全面的、面向机制的ILE分类法，从意义编码与恢复的底层操作角度进行分类，而非基于交际目标。将该分类法融入LLM提示中，与四种现有分类法及无分类法基线进行对比评估，使用2000条人工标注的TikTok和Bluesky帖子作为测试数据。

Result: 所提分类法在三款LLM上均取得最强的文档级和片段级性能，相较于最佳基准，准确率提升4.7%，F1提升5.4%。

Conclusion: 一个全面的、面向机制的ILE分类法可作为检测新兴编码语言的稳定支架，并为内容审核提供有效输入，实证结果验证了其在跨模型场景下的鲁棒性和优越性。

Abstract: To avoid moderation and surveillance on social media, some users routinely invent indirect linguistic expressions (ILE) that camouflage sensitive meanings. Such expressions surface as algospeak, euphemisms, and adversarial obfuscation, depending on intent and context, and they involve recurring encoding mechanisms. We propose a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy of ILE that abstracts away from communicative goals and instead categorizes the underlying operations through which meaning is encoded and recovered. We evaluate the taxonomy by incorporating it into LLM prompts and comparing it with four existing taxonomies and a no-taxonomy baseline, using 2,000 manually annotated TikTok and Bluesky posts. The proposed taxonomy attains the strongest document- and span-level performance across the three LLMs, achieving an improvement of 4.7% in accuracy and 5.4% in F1 over the best-performing benchmark. The empirical results reveal the importance of a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy as a stable scaffold for detecting emerging coded language and a useful input to content moderation. Disclaimer: This paper contains content that may be profane, vulgar, or offensive.

</details>


### [160] [LLM-Based Examination of Eligibility Criteria from Securities Prospectuses at the German Central Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27316)
*Serhii Hamotskyi,Akash Kumar Gautam,Christian Hänig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将大语言模型（LLM）应用于德国央行抵押品合格性审核，提出了一种生成式信息抽取流程，将任务分解为抽取、规范化和解释三个阶段，并引入基于LLM-as-a-judge的语义评估方法，最终在文档级合格性判断上达到91%的高精度，展现出保守、低误收的运行特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国央行需要审核证券作为抵押品的合格性，但手动从冗长、半结构化且常为德英双语的法律招股说明书中提取关键信息极为耗时。传统NER方法受限于OCR噪声、语言差异、僵化的span边界约束以及需要大量人工标注数据，难以胜任该任务。

Method: 采用大语言模型构建生成式信息抽取管线，将任务分解为三步：信息抽取（extraction）、规范化（normalization）和解释（interpretation），从而灵活应对噪声文本和德英混合内容。评估方面，提出基于LLM-as-a-judge的语义评估方法，替代传统的位置匹配指标。

Result: LLM系统在文档级合格性判断上达到最高91%的精确率，并表现出保守的运行特性，有效降低了误收（false acceptance）风险。

Conclusion: 将LLM应用于抵押品合格性审核是可行且有效的，生成式信息抽取管线配合语义评估方法显著优于传统NER方案，尤其适合处理噪声多、多语混杂的金融法律文书。

Abstract: Verifying the eligibility of securities as collateral is a key responsibility of the German Central Bank. However, manually verifying these assets against legal and financial criteria within lengthy, semi-structured, and often bilingual prospectuses is a resource-intensive task. While previous efforts utilized traditional Named Entity Recognition (NER) for information extraction, these methods can struggle with OCR noise, linguistic variance, and rigid span-based constraints, and the need for manually annotated training data for each relevant annotation type. In this paper, we present the first case study applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to the eligibility examination process, shifting the paradigm toward a generative Information Extraction pipeline. Our approach decomposes the task into extraction, normalization, and interpretation, allowing for greater flexibility in handling noisy text and interleaved German-English content. We further introduce a value-based evaluation methodology using LLM-as-a-judge, which offers a more semantic assessment than location-based metrics. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based systems achieve high precision (up to 91%) in document-level eligibility, exhibiting a conservative operating profile that minimizes false acceptance.

</details>


### [161] [Empowering GUI Agents via Autonomous Experience Exploration and Hindsight Experience Utilization for Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27330)
*Tianyi Men,Zhuoran Jin,Pengfei Cao,Yubo Chen,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PEEU方法，通过自主探索环境发现经验并利用后见之明合成高层级训练数据，以增强小型多模态大语言模型的跨网站任务规划能力；同时提出TDHAF框架，系统分析不同任务粒度下的组合泛化行为，揭示高层级任务训练对OOD泛化的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开源MLLMs虽然成本低且保护隐私，但在网页GUI任务中存在规划能力弱、跨网站泛化不足的问题，亟需一种方法提升其复杂任务分解和OOD泛化能力。

Method: 提出PEEU（Planning Experience Exploration and Utilization）方法：自主探索环境发现经验，利用后见之明经验（hindsight experience）合成严格对齐的高层级训练数据。同时提出TDHAF（Task Decomposition Hierarchical Analysis Framework），从低、中、高三个任务粒度系统研究组合泛化行为。

Result: 7B模型达到30.6%准确率，超越更大的Qwen2.5-VL-32B模型。分析表明：掌握低层原子技能并不能保证高层规划能力，而高层任务训练能带来更强的OOD泛化性能。

Conclusion: 构建后见之明高层级任务并充分利用经验，对小型MLLMs的OOD规划能力至关重要。PEEU方法有效弥补了小模型与商业大模型之间的差距。

Abstract: Multimodal web agents can assist humans in operating repetitive GUI tasks, where effective task planning is essential for decomposing complex tasks into executable actions. While small open source MLLMs are cost efficient and privacy preserving compared with commercial large models, they suffer from weak planning and limited cross website generalization. To address these limitations, we introduce the planning experience exploration and utilization (PEEU) method, which autonomously explores environments to discover experiences and utilizes hindsight experience to synthesize strictly aligned, high level training data. To quantitatively analyze the generalization behaviors driving this performance, we propose the task decomposition hierarchical analysis framework (TDHAF) to systematically study compositional generalization across three task granularities: low, middle and high levels. Our analysis reveals that mastering low level atomic skills does not guarantee high level planning competence, while high level task training yields stronger OOD generalization. Experiments on real world benchmarks demonstrate PEEU's superior effectiveness: our 7B model achieves 30.6% accuracy, outperforming the much larger Qwen2.5-VL-32B model. These demonstrate constructing hindsight high level tasks and leveraging experiences is crucial for OOD planning abilities of small MLLMs.

</details>


### [162] [Mapping Political-Elite Networks in Europe with a Multilingual Joint Entity-Relation Extraction Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27347)
*Kirill Solovev,Jana Lasser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套完全开源的、多语言的、从大规模非结构化新闻语料中构建带符号时序知识图谱的模块化流水线，结合了基于span的命名实体识别、三级实体链接级联以及基于本体约束的混合专家关系抽取模型，并在奥地利和波兰两个案例研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治精英究竟形成寻租联盟还是公民网络，是比政领域的核心问题。然而，大规模观察这些复杂、非正式、对抗性的关系历来依赖密集的人工编码，传统自动文本分析方法局限于简单共现，而近期LLM方法多依赖闭源API、缺乏跨语言能力且实体解析难以规模化。因此亟需一个开源、可复现、跨语言的大规模关系抽取框架。

Method: 提出一套模块化的完全开源流水线：(1) 基于span的命名实体识别（NER）；(2) 三级链接级联，将mention映射到语言无关的Wikidata标识符；(3) 高吞吐量、本体约束的混合专家（MoE）模型，通过引导式解码提取有向、带符号的关系，并基于领域本体进行约束；(4) 对整个流程在3491条关系的金标准上进行全覆盖抽样检验（68.2%严格正确率，93.7%宽松正确率）

Result: 两项大规模案例研究验证了流水线：在奥地利语料中，重建了一个政党从分裂、人员流向后续派系到法院定罪的完整生命周期；在波兰语料中，揭示了国有企业庇护的重叠经济与治理网络，以及公民纲领党（PO）与法律与公正党（PiS）两极化双头垄断下结构均衡的带符号冲突网络。

Conclusion: 该框架通过桥接原始多语言文本与结构化关系数据，为跨国实证计算社会科学提供了一个稳健、可复现的基础设施，有望推动比政领域对精英网络的大规模、跨语言实证研究。

Abstract: Whether political elites organise into rent-seeking coalitions that capture public resources or civic networks that sustain governance is a central question in comparative politics. Yet observing these complex, informal, and adversarial ties at scale has historically required intensive manual coding, while automated text-as-data methods have largely been limited to simple co-occurrence. Recent large language model (LLM) approaches offer a path forward but often rely on proprietary APIs, lack cross-lingual capability, and struggle with scalable entity resolution. We present a modular, fully open-weight pipeline for multilingual joint entity-relation extraction that builds signed, temporal knowledge graphs from massive unstructured news corpora. It combines span-based named-entity recognition (NER) with a three-stage linking cascade mapping mentions to language-independent Wikidata identifiers; a high-throughput, ontology-constrained mixture-of-experts model then uses guided decoding to extract directed, signed relationships grounded in a domain ontology. A full-coverage spot-check against a 3491-relation gold standard shows high textual correctness (68.2% strict to 93.7% lenient). Two large-scale case studies validate the pipeline against the public record. In Austria, it reconstructs a political party's complete lifecycle, dating internal fractures and tracking personnel into successor factions and court convictions. In a Polish corpus, it uncovers the overlapping economic and governance networks of state-enterprise patronage, alongside the structurally balanced, signed conflict network of the polarized Civic Platform (Platforma Obywatelska, PO)--Law and Justice (Prawo i Sprawiedliwość, PiS) duopoly. By bridging raw multilingual text and structured relational data, our framework provides a robust, replicable foundation for cross-national empirical computational social science.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Physics-guided Convolutional Neural Network for Domain Growth Prediction in Systems with Conserved Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26128)
*Vijay Yadav,Madhu Priya,Manish Dev Shrimali,Prabhat K. Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于注意力机制和物理引导的卷积神经网络替代模型，用于学习Cahn-Hilliard方程描述的二元混合物相分离微观结构演化，能够长时间稳定精确预测，并保持守恒动力学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理、化学和生物系统由非线性偏微分方程描述，传统数值求解器计算成本高昂，需要开发高效的深度学习替代模型来加速微观结构演化的模拟。

Method: 提出一种基于注意力机制（attention-based）且受物理引导（physics-guided）的卷积神经网络替代模型。模型以Cahn-Hilliard方程为物理约束，学习二元混合物相分离的完整时间演化过程，涵盖临界和偏临界混合物两种情形。

Result: （1）模型在长时间 rollout 预测中保持稳定性和准确性；（2）预测结果在演化全过程中保持混合物组成守恒；（3）能准确捕捉畴尺寸增长，并与 Lifshitz-Slyozov 畴增长定律一致。

Conclusion: 所提框架对具有守恒动力学的系统建模效果显著，且可扩展至其他复杂动力系统。

Abstract: The spatiotemporal evolution of many physical, chemical, and biological systems is described by nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep neural network-based surrogate models have gained increasing interest as efficient alternatives to computationally expensive traditional numerical solvers. In this work, we propose an attention-based, physics-guided convolutional neural network as a surrogate model to learn the microstructural evolution of such systems. We train the model to accurately predict the full time-evolution of phase separation in binary mixtures governed by the Cahn-Hilliard equation. We show that predictions from our trained surrogate model remain stable and accurate over long-time rollouts for both critical and off-critical mixtures and preserve the mixture composition throughout evolution. We also show that our model accurately captures the growth of domain size and is consistent with the Lifshitz-Slyozov domain-growth law. The prediction results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for modeling systems with conserved kinetics and can be extended to other complex dynamical systems.

</details>


### [164] [\chisao{}: A GPU-Native Parallel Optimizer for Multimodal Black-Box Functions via Convergence-Anticonvergence Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26164)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChiSAO 是一种GPU原生的群体优化算法，通过"收敛-停止-反转-粘滞-振荡"循环实现多模态黑箱函数的全模式发现，在SFU基准测试中实现100%模式恢复率，并在维度≥8的困难多模态函数上显著超越所有CPU基线方法，获得高达34倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态优化方法（basin-hopping、CMA-ES、multistart梯度下降）均为串行执行，无法利用现代GPU的大规模并行能力。亟需一种能充分利用GPU并行性、同时可靠发现所有模态的优化算法。

Method: 提出ChiSAO（Convergence-Halt-Invert-Stick-And-Oscillate），核心机制包括：(1) 非对称结构移动：达到真实峰值的样本被"冻结"保存，其余样本通过动量驱动的反收敛和随机平滑梯度继续探索；(2) 收敛-反收敛振荡循环：刻意制造收敛与发散交替的动力学来逃离局部陷阱；(3) 自适应重播种：采用Repulse Monkey和Golden Rooster两种互补策略维持种群多样性。所有梯度通过有限差分获得，为无导数优化场景。

Result: 在SFU优化基准套件全部42个函数上（维度d∈{2,4,8,16,32,64}），ChiSAO实现100%模式恢复率，而所有CPU基线方法在d≥8的最困难多模态函数上均失败。在Michalewicz d=64上较basin-hopping加速34倍，在单模态Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid d=64上加速39倍。在似然噪声σ_noise高达1.0时仍保持100%模式检测可靠性。

Conclusion: ChiSAO证明，通过GPU原生并行设计配合收敛-反收敛振荡机制，可以在无导数条件下以100%可靠性发现多模态函数的所有模式，同时获得数量级的加速，且对噪声具有高度鲁棒性。算法已作为开源Python包发布在PyPI。

Abstract: Finding all modes of a multimodal black-box function is a fundamental challenge in optimization, Bayesian inference, and scientific computing. Existing approaches -- basin-hopping, CMA-ES, multistart gradient descent -- operate sequentially and cannot exploit the massive parallelism of modern GPU hardware. We introduce \chisao{} (\textbf{C}onvergence-\textbf{H}alt-\textbf{I}nvert-\textbf{S}tick-\textbf{A}nd-\textbf{O}scillate), a GPU-native population optimizer that runs an entire sample batch simultaneously and exploits a deliberate convergence-anticonvergence oscillation cycle to escape local traps while freezing confirmed modes. The structural move is asymmetric: samples that reach true peaks are frozen (``stuck'') and preserved, while the rest keep exploring via momentum-based anti-convergence and stochastically smoothed gradients. Adaptive reseeding via two complementary strategies (Repulse Monkey and Golden Rooster) maintains population diversity throughout. On all 42 functions of the Simon Fraser University optimization benchmark suite across dimensions $d \in \{2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64\}$, \chisao{} achieves \textbf{100\%} mode recovery where all CPU baselines collapse at $d \geq 8$ on the hardest multimodal functions, at up to \textbf{$34\times$} speedup over basin-hopping on functions where all methods succeed (Michalewicz $d=64$) and up to \textbf{$39\times$} on unimodal functions (Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid $d=64$, pure GPU dividend). All benchmarks evaluate the objective by value alone -- gradients come from finite differences -- so the reported speedups are a derivative-free worst case. Under substantial likelihood noise ($σ_{\mathrm{noise}}$ up to 1.0), mode detection remains 100\% reliable. The algorithm is available as a standalone open-source Python package on PyPI.

</details>


### [165] [Implementation of reinforcement learning in chemical reaction networks: application to phototaxis as curiosity-driven exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26168)
*Ruyi Tang,Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Colliaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）与生化反应动力学相结合的计算框架，将单细胞藻类的趋光行为重新定义为信息驱动的感觉运动过程。通过在30条莱茵衣藻轨迹上进行逆强化学习，模型成功复现了经验性的趋光对齐分布，并将"run-tumble"交替解释为一种主动的信息获取策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对单细胞藻类趋光性的建模多采用机械性的"run-tumble"刺激-响应规则，忽略了生物体如何主动采样环境以降低感觉模糊性。作者从最小认知视角出发，希望将这种导航行为重新框架化为一种主观的、信息驱动的感觉运动过程。

Method: 提出POMDP与生化反应动力学耦合框架：环境变量设为隐变量，细胞通过无记忆贝叶斯步骤更新最小内部状态；构建CRN-ODE实现趋光与探索性重定向的平衡；设计生物物理感光观测过程及信息增益的化学可计算多项式界；使用逆强化学习（IRL）在30条实验记录的衣藻轨迹上推断行为目标，并以SSA为基准进行验证。

Result: 模型成功复现了经验性的趋光对齐分布，与目标导向的SSA基线表现相当。run-tumble交替作为一种信息获取策略涌现出来：tumble使细胞重定向以采样新的感觉配置，从而解决传感器模糊性。

Conclusion: 细胞内生化网络可以支持自适应信息寻求行为，实现细胞导航。该框架展示了如何从最小认知角度理解单细胞生物的感觉运动决策，为连接生化反应动力学与认知过程提供了桥梁。

Abstract: Living systems navigate environments using noisy and incomplete sensory signals. In unicellular algae, phototaxis is often modeled as a mechanistic run--tumble process driven by stimulus--response rules. However, such descriptions overlook how organisms actively sample their environment to reduce sensory ambiguity. From a minimal cognition perspective, we reframe this navigation as a subjective, information-driven sensorimotor process. To this end, we propose a framework linking a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) with biochemical reaction dynamics. Environmental variables are hidden, while the cell updates a minimal internal state from each observation through a memoryless Bayesian step. These internal dynamics balance orienting toward light with exploratory reorientation and can be implemented through Chemical-Reaction-Network Ordinary Differential Equations (CRN--ODEs). Our model includes a biophysical observation process for photoreception and a chemically computable polynomial bound on information gain. Using Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) on 30 experimentally recorded Chlamydomonas trajectories, we infer the behavioral objective consistent with observed phototactic motion and benchmark the resulting dynamics with standard Stochastic Simulation Algorithm (SSA) baselines. Our model reproduces the empirical alignment-to-light distribution, comparable to objective SSA baselines on this dataset. Within this framework, run--tumble alternation emerges as an information-acquisition strategy: tumbling reorients the cell to sample new sensory configurations and resolve sensor ambiguity, demonstrating how intracellular biochemical networks can support adaptive information-seeking behavior in cellular navigation.

</details>


### [166] [Statistical and Structural Approaches to Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26200)
*Antonio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当代算法公平范式的两个根本局限：依赖确定性点估计进行审计，以及将个体视为脱离结构背景的孤立实体。论文旨在识别并解决这些问题，以提升公平性方法在复杂社会技术环境中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习系统已演变为调节人类机会的社会技术架构，但优化预测精度的模型可能系统性损害边缘群体。早期公平性缓解策略基于脆弱的简化假设，在复杂社会技术环境中效果有限，因此需要识别并超越这些范式的根本局限。

Method: 本论文采用批判性分析与理论建构的方法，识别出两大核心问题——审计中确定性点估计的局限性，以及个体分析中结构背景的缺失，并针对这些局限提出相应的解决路径。

Result: 论文诊断出当代算法公平的两大关键缺陷：(1) 依赖确定性点估计进行公平性审计，忽略了预测不确定性；(2) 将个体原子化处理，忽视其所处的结构性背景与社会关系网络。

Conclusion: 为构建真正有效的算法公平框架，必须超越确定性点估计审计和原子化个体观这两大简化假设，转而纳入预测不确定性以及个体的结构性嵌入特征，从而在复杂社会技术系统中实现更稳健的公平性保障。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems have outgrown their origins as isolated predictive constructs, evolving into complex socio-technical architectures that actively mediate human opportunity. As algorithms increasingly determine access to economic and social opportunities, it has become widely recognized that these systems are deeply embedded with the structural inequalities and prejudices of their environments. The field of algorithmic fairness emerged in response to the growing recognition that models optimized for predictive accuracy can systematically disadvantage marginalized groups. Early mitigation strategies, however, rested on fragile simplifications that limited their effectiveness in complex socio-technical environments. This thesis identifies and addresses two fundamental limitations of contemporary fairness paradigms: the reliance on deterministic point estimates for auditing and the treatment of individuals as isolated entities devoid of structural context.

</details>


### [167] [Neural Architecture Search for Generative Adversarial Networks: A Comprehensive Review and Critical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26169)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了神经架构搜索（NAS）在生成对抗网络（GAN）设计中的应用，对现有NAS-GAN方法按搜索策略和评估指标进行分类比较，指出进化算法和梯度方法在特定场景下的优势，并强调需要超越IS和FID的鲁棒评估指标及多样化数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAN的手工架构设计面临诸多挑战，而NAS能够自动化搜索高效架构。然而，当前缺乏对NAS在GAN中应用的系统性梳理和比较，研究人员需要一个结构化的综述来了解各类方法的优劣，以指导后续更有效的NAS-GAN方法开发。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，对现有NAS-GAN技术按搜索策略（如进化算法、梯度方法等）、评估指标（IS、FID等）和性能结果等维度进行分类、比较与结构化分析。

Result: 主要发现包括：进化算法和基于梯度的方法在特定场景下表现优越；传统评估指标（IS、FID）存在局限，需要更鲁棒的评估体系；GAN性能评估需依托更多样化的数据集。

Conclusion: NAS能显著提升GAN的性能、稳定性和效率。未来研究应聚焦于开发更鲁棒的评估指标、扩展数据多样性，以及进一步优化搜索策略。本文的结构化比较为后续NAS-GAN研究提供了路线图。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has emerged as a pivotal technique in optimizing the design of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), automating the search for effective architectures while addressing the challenges inherent in manual design. This paper provides a comprehensive review of NAS methods applied to GANs, categorizing and comparing various approaches based on criteria such as search strategies, evaluation metrics, and performance outcomes. The review highlights the benefits of NAS in improving GAN performance, stability, and efficiency, while also identifying limitations and areas for future research. Key findings include the superiority of evolutionary algorithms and gradient-based methods in certain contexts, the importance of robust evaluation metrics beyond traditional scores like Inception Score (IS) and Fréchet Inception Distance (FID), and the need for diverse datasets in assessing GAN performance. By presenting a structured comparison of existing NAS-GAN techniques, this paper aims to guide researchers in developing more effective NAS methods and advancing the field of GANs.

</details>


### [168] [KG-TRACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Mechanistic Grounding in Antimicrobial Resistance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26179)
*Naman Garg,Sarika Jain,Sourav Yadav,Bharat K. Bhargava,Ghanapriya Singh,Abhishek Srivastava,Parimal Kar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KG-TRACE 是一种神经符号框架，将 WHO 突变知识图谱作为结构化生物学约束整合到基因组模型中，通过认知信任门动态融合神经网络预测与符号知识，在结核分枝杆菌耐药性预测中实现了 92.5% 的符号覆盖率，并提出了生物接地理比率（BGR）来量化神经归因与已知生物学的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于全基因组测序的抗生素耐药性（AMR）预测模型虽准确率高，但缺乏将神经网络归因锚定于已确立生物学通路中的机制，导致临床信任度不足。需要一种能够提供可验证审计追踪的方案，弥合预测准确性与临床信任之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出 KG-TRACE 神经符号框架：(1) 将 WHO 突变知识图谱作为结构化生物学约束；(2) 利用 RotatE 模型生成知识图谱嵌入；(3) 通过可学习的认知信任门（epistemic trust gate）动态加权神经证据与符号生物学知识，实现基因组特征与 KG 嵌入的融合；(4) 引入生物接地理比率（BGR）作为数据集级别指标，量化神经归因与已知生物学的对齐程度，并对不确定病例发出实验室随访标志。

Result: 在 CRyPTIC 结核分枝杆菌队列上评估，对异烟肼的 AUROC 达 0.9760，具有竞争性准确率；实现了 92.5% 的异烟肼耐药预测符号覆盖率；能有效识别耐多药（MDR）共现伪影，并对 "UNCERTAIN" 病例发出实验室随访标志。

Conclusion: KG-TRACE 的核心价值在于符号接地理而非单纯提升预测精度。神经符号接地理为临床医生提供了可验证的审计追踪，证明将结构化生物学知识融入深度学习模型是弥合预测准确性与临床信任之间差距的有效路径。

Abstract: While WGS-based AMR prediction has reached high accuracy, existing models lack a mechanism to ground neural attributions in established biological pathways. We present KG-TRACE, a novel neuro-symbolic framework that integrates the WHO mutation knowledge graph (KG) as a structured biological constraint on a neural genomic model. Unlike existing methods that learn statistical patterns in isolation, KG-TRACE fuses genomic features and RotatE-based KG embeddings through a learned epistemic trust gate, dynamically weighting neural evidence against symbolic biological knowledge.
  Evaluated on the CRyPTIC M. tuberculosis cohort, KG-TRACE achieves an AUROC of 0.9760 for isoniazid, achieving competitive accuracy while its primary value lies in symbolic grounding, not predictive uplift. More importantly, we introduce the Biological Grounding Ratio (BGR), a dataset-level metric that quantifies alignment between neural attributions and established biology. Our framework achieves a 92.5% symbolic coverage of isoniazid-resistant predictions and effectively identifies MDR co-occurrence artifacts by issuing laboratory follow-up flags for 'UNCERTAIN' cases. We demonstrate that neuro-symbolic grounding provides a verifiable audit trail for clinicians, bridging the gap between predictive accuracy and clinical trust.

</details>


### [169] [Learning Probabilistic Filters with Strictly Proper Scoring Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26497)
*Eviatar Bach,Ricardo Baptista,Jochen Bröcker,Bohan Chen,Andrew Stuart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于严格适当评分规则（proper scoring rules）训练的新型集合数据同化方法 PSEF，使用合成状态-观测轨迹以在线方式学习逼近贝叶斯滤波分布，在非线性、非高斯和多模态场景下显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯滤波分布是动力系统状态不确定性量化的自然对象，但极少能作为监督学习目标。然而，可利用预报模型生成合成系统轨迹及合成观测，这为基于模拟数据训练滤波方法提供了可能。现有基于均方误差的学习方法难以准确捕捉完整的概率分布结构。

Method: 提出 Proper Scoring Ensemble Filter (PSEF)：分析步骤由一个置换不变的Transformer映射表示，输入预报集合与观测，输出分析集合；训练使用严格适当评分规则（具体采用energy score），奖励整个概率分布的准确性；理论证明在可实现假设下，总体目标函数由真贝叶斯滤波分布最小化；推导了有限集合经验目标，并通过平均场一致性论证将其与总体目标关联。

Result: 数值实验表明：PSEF能准确逼近非线性、非高斯、多模态等具有挑战性的滤波分布；在数据同化任务中性能优于经典方法和基于均方误差目标的学习方法；对近高斯问题，学习对EnKF的修正效果最佳；对高度非高斯问题，端到端方法（舍弃EnKF归纳偏置）更优。

Conclusion: PSEF通过严格适当评分规则训练置换不变Transformer，成功实现了仅依赖合成数据的贝叶斯滤波分布逼近，在多种复杂滤波场景中展现出优越性能，并揭示了不同非高斯程度下最优架构选择的规律。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering of partially and noisily observed dynamical systems seeks to infer the evolving conditional distribution of the state of a dynamical system, given observations, in an online fashion. This Bayesian filtering distribution is the natural object for uncertainty quantification, but it is rarely available as a supervised learning target. However, one can often use the forecast model to generate synthetic system trajectories, along with synthetic observations. We introduce the proper scoring ensemble filter (PSEF), an ensemble data assimilation method based on training an analysis map to approximate the filtering distribution using only synthetic state--observation trajectories. The analysis step is represented as a permutation-invariant, transformer-based map that takes as input a forecast ensemble and observations, producing an analysis ensemble. Training is based on strictly proper scoring rules -- with the energy score used in our implementation -- so that probabilistic accuracy is rewarded over the whole probability distribution. We prove that, under a realizability assumption, the population objective is minimized by the true Bayesian filtering distribution. We also derive the finite-ensemble empirical objective used in training and relate its single state--observation trajectory form to the population objective, using a mean-field consistency argument. Numerical experiments show that the learned filter accurately approximates challenging filtering distributions, including nonlinear, non-Gaussian, and multi-modal posteriors, and achieves stronger performance in data assimilation tasks than classical methods or learning-based methods with mean-squared-error objectives. For close-to-Gaussian problems, learning a correction to the EnKF is the best approach, while for highly non-Gaussian problems an end-to-end approach that discards this inductive bias is superior.

</details>


### [170] [Necessary but Not Sufficient: Temperature Control and Reproducibility in LLM-as-Judge Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26185)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了LLM-as-judge评估框架中一个被忽视的结构性问题：即使将采样温度设为0，评判器的打分仍不可复现。作者在日本AISI的aisev安全评估代码库上验证，发现(1)默认未设置温度导致使用API默认值1.0，边界案例翻转率高达~50%；(2)即使强制temperature=0且top_k=1，部分边界案例仍不可复现；(3)Claude Opus新版本已弃用温度参数，使主要缓解措施失效。作者呼吁将评判器不一致性作为一等健康指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-judge评估范式普遍假设"temperature=0即确定性评分"，且安全评估中pass/fail判定直接影响模型部署决策。但缺乏对这一假设的系统性验证，尤其在生产级安全评估代码库和最新模型API上的测试。

Method: 以日本AISI开源安全评估框架aisev为实验平台，设计690次API调用，覆盖2个提供商(Anthropic/OpenAI)、3个模型层级、5种采样配置（含默认temperature、temperature=0、top_k=1等），对相同输入进行20次重复运行，逐项统计pass/fail翻转率。

Result: 两层面发现：(1)框架未设置temperature/seed时，API默认temperature=1.0，边界项目跨运行不一致率高达~50%；(2)强制temperature=0+top_k=1后，7个边界项目中仍有1-2个不可复现；(3)Claude Opus 4.7/4.8已完全弃用temperature参数，主流缓解策略失效。

Conclusion: 评估框架仅报告单次运行结果会系统性地将噪声伪装成安全属性。建议所有评估框架将评判器不一致性(grader disagreement)作为与评分本身同等重要的一等健康指标纳入报告，并公开方差估计。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge ("grader") components are now standard in evaluation harnesses, including safety evaluations where a pass/fail verdict may gate downstream deployment decisions. A widespread assumption is that setting the grader's sampling temperature to 0 makes grading deterministic. We test this assumption against a real safety-evaluation codebase (Japan AISI's open-source aisev) and show it fails on two levels. First, the harness invokes its grader without setting temperature or seed; the underlying provider silently applies its default of 1.0, so items near the decision boundary flip pass/fail across identical runs (per-item disagreement up to ~50% over 20 runs). Second, pinning temperature=0 reduces but does not eliminate flips: across 690 API calls spanning two providers, three model tiers, and five sampling configurations, 1-2 of 7 borderline items remain non-reproducible even under forced greedy decoding (top_k=1). Claude Opus 4.7/4.8 has since deprecated temperature entirely, rendering the primary mitigation inapplicable to newer model generations. These findings expose a structural gap: evaluation harnesses that report single-run verdicts without variance or grader-disagreement metrics can present noise as a safety property. We release a reproduction harness (690 calls, 7 conditions) and recommend that harnesses treat grader disagreement as a first-class health metric alongside the scores themselves.

</details>


### [171] [Clue-Guided Money Laundering Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26189)
*Boyang Wang,Jianing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线索引导的洗钱团伙发现框架Clue2Group，从初始线索出发，通过构建局部调查上下文、多语义局部-时序GNN估计风险场，并融合风险、结构与先验模式证据，渐进式恢复完整洗钱团伙结构，弥补现有图异常检测与真实反洗钱调查流程之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法仅输出节点级风险警报，全局团伙发现方法则被动搜索全网可疑群体，两者均与真实反洗钱调查流程不匹配——真实调查中分析师通常从具体线索出发逐步扩展以还原责任团伙。因此需要一种从初始线索出发、通过人机交互渐进恢复团伙的新范式。

Method: 提出Clue-Guided Group Discovery（CGGD）任务范式，并设计Clue2Group框架：1）构建紧凑的局部调查上下文以降低噪声并保留链式与环式洗钱结构；2）使用多语义局部-时序图神经网络（GNN）估计线索条件化的局部风险场；3）综合风险证据、结构证据和先验模式证据，恢复连贯的洗钱团伙结构。整个过程支持分析师交互式逐步扩展。

Result: 在两个大规模反洗钱基准数据集上的实验表明，Clue2Group提供了一种实用的线索驱动分析框架，有效支持真实AML调查场景下的团伙发现任务，显著优于现有节点级异常检测和全局团伙发现方法。

Conclusion: Clue2Group成功弥合了图基AML研究与真实调查工作流之间的差距，为反洗钱领域提供了一种从线索出发、交互式、可落地的团伙发现解决方案，是图学习方法走向实际AML调查的关键一步。

Abstract: Money Laundering Group Discovery (MLGD) aims to identify hidden criminal groups and recover their complete structures in large-scale financial networks. Existing graph anomaly detection methods mainly produce node-level risk alerts, while global group discovery methods passively search for suspicious groups over the whole network. Both are mismatched with real Anti-money-laundering (AML) investigations, where analysts usually start from a concrete clue and gradually expand the investigation to recover the responsible group. To address this gap, we propose Clue-Guided Group Discovery (CGGD), where a laundering group is progressively recovered from an initial clue set through analyst interaction. We further propose Clue2Group, a framework that first constructs a compact local investigation context to reduce noise and preserve chain-like and cycle-like laundering structures. It then estimates a clue-conditioned local risk field with a multi-semantic local-temporal GNN, and finally integrates risk, structural, and prior-pattern evidence to recover a coherent laundering group. Experiments on two large-scale AML benchmarks show that Clue2Group provides a practical clue-driven analysis framework for AML investigations, offering a feasible step toward bridging the gap between graph-based AML research and real investigation workflows.

</details>


### [172] [Escaping Iterative Parameter-Space Noise: Differentially Private Learning with a Hypernetwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26772)
*Naoki Nishikawa,Shokichi Takakura,Satoshi Hasegawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的差分隐私学习框架，用超网络将私有数据集映射到模型参数，仅对低维数据集嵌入注入一次性噪声，显著降低噪声影响，在扩散模型LoRA微调上优于DP-SGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DP-SGD在训练过程中反复向高维参数空间注入噪声，噪声量过大严重损害模型效用。需要一种避免参数空间迭代优化的新DP学习范式。

Method: 提出基于超网络的DP学习框架：先在公开数据集上训练一个超网络；对私有数据，将每个样本嵌入低维表示，聚合后注入噪声得到差分隐私数据集嵌入，超网络据此生成目标模型参数。噪声仅注入一次到低维表示中。

Result: 理论分析表明，在固定隐私预算下，该方法比DP-SGD获得更高效用。在扩散模型LoRA微调实验上，FID指标优于DP-SGD及其他基于公开数据引导的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将隐私噪声限制在低维数据集嵌入的单次注入，该框架有效缓解了DP训练中的噪声累积问题，为差分隐私深度学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) training of neural networks is often hindered by the large amount of noise required by gradient-based methods such as DP-SGD, which repeatedly inject high-dimensional noise in parameter space throughout training. In this paper, we propose a new framework for DP learning that avoids iterative optimization in parameter space. Instead of updating the target model using privatized gradients, we employ a hypernetwork trained on public datasets to map a private dataset to the parameters of the target model. Specifically, each example is embedded into a low-dimensional representation, the embeddings are aggregated and perturbed to obtain a DP dataset embedding, and the hypernetwork generates the target model parameters from this noisy embedding. Because privacy noise is injected only once into a low-dimensional dataset representation, our approach can significantly reduce the adverse effect of noise. We theoretically show in a synthetic setting that, under a fixed privacy budget, models produced by our approach achieve higher utility than those trained with DP-SGD. Moreover, we apply our approach to LoRA fine-tuning of diffusion models and show that it achieves lower FID than LoRA models trained with DP-SGD and other public-data-guided methods.

</details>


### [173] [Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26192)
*Jialan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦哈希投影潜在因子模型（FHPLF），将哈希学习与联邦学习结合，通过二值梯度矩阵替代实值梯度、投影汉明距离建模相似性、以及安全二值梯度重组与隐私增强上传策略，在降低通信开销和增强隐私保护的同时提升了表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈希学习需要集中上传用户数据，违反数据安全法规；联邦学习虽能保护隐私，但传输大规模实值梯度带来高通信开销和隐私风险。现有联邦哈希学习方法中，二值码表示能力有限，导致模型精度下降。因此需要一种兼顾精度、效率和隐私的联邦哈希学习方案。

Method: 提出FHPLF模型，包含三项创新：(1) 用二值梯度类矩阵替代实值梯度矩阵，降低计算、存储和通信成本并增强隐私；(2) 采用投影汉明距离进行相似性建模，捕获单个比特的重要性以提升表示能力；(3) 提出安全二值梯度重组与隐私增强上传（SBG-PEU）策略，进一步降低传输中用户交互泄露风险。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，FHPLF一致优于现有最先进的哈希学习和联邦学习方法，在精度、效率和隐私保护之间实现了良好权衡。

Conclusion: FHPLF成功将哈希学习集成到联邦学习框架中，解决了二值码表示能力不足的问题，同时显著降低了通信开销并增强了隐私保护，在多个数据集上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Hash Learning (HL) is an efficient representation learning approach that maps real-valued data into compact binary representations. Traditional HL methods typically require users to upload personal data to a central server, which is incompatible with increasingly stringent data security regulations. Federated Learning (FL) provides a decentralized paradigm for learning globally optimal models without centralizing private data. However, most FL methods rely on transmitting large-scale real-valued gradient information, leading to high communication overhead and potential privacy risks. Integrating HL into FL is a promising solution. Nevertheless, existing HL methods suffer from limited representational capacity of binary codes, which may degrade model accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose a Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor (FHPLF) model. FHPLF introduces three key innovations: (a) replacing real-valued gradient matrices with binary gradient-like matrices, significantly reducing computation, storage, and communication costs while enhancing privacy protection; (b) leveraging Projected Hamming Distance for similarity modeling, which captures the importance of individual binary bits to improve representation capability; and (c) proposing a Secure Binary Gradient Reassembly and Privacy-Enhanced Upload (SBG-PEU) strategy to further reduce the risk of user interaction leakage during transmission. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that FHPLF consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HL and FL methods, achieving a favorable trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and privacy preservation.

</details>


### [174] [Asymptotically Optimal Learning for Parametric Prophet Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26893)
*Jung-hun Kim,Anna Grebennikova,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有未知参数θ的指数型参数族（包括指数分布、Pareto分布和有界支持幂族分布）i.i.d.奖励的先知不等式在线学习问题。作者首先刻画了该族的最优全信息渐近竞争比，然后提出了一种基于置信区间的动态规划策略，仅利用在线观测即可达到相同的最优渐近竞争比，无需外部离线样本，并给出了典型分布的具体收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先知不等式（prophet inequalities）中，奖励分布通常假设为已知。然而在实际应用中，决策者往往对潜在分布缺乏先验知识。本文旨在弥合这一差距，研究当奖励分布来自指数型参数族但参数θ未知时，如何仅通过在线观测实现渐近最优的竞争比，使在线学习策略的性能能够匹配全信息下的理论最优界。

Method: 作者首先对指数型参数族（包含指数分布、Pareto分布和有界支持幂族分布）推导了全信息下的渐近竞争比的精确表达式：无界支持情形下极限为$(θ/(θ-c_+))^{c_+/θ}/Γ(1-c_+/θ)$，有界支持情形下极限为1。在此基础上，提出了一种基于置信度的动态规划（confidence-based dynamic programming）在线学习策略，该策略利用参数族显式的结构形式构建置信区间，在每一轮决策中通过动态规划求解最优阈值，无需依赖外部离线样本即可自适应地逼近未知参数θ。

Result: 所提出的基于置信度的动态规划策略被证明能够仅使用在线观测达到与全信息情形相同的渐近最优竞争比。对于典型分布（如指数分布、Pareto分布等），作者进一步推导了分布特定的收敛速率。合成数据的数值实验验证了算法的实际表现。

Conclusion: 本文完整地解决了指数型参数族中未知参数下先知不等式的在线学习问题：既给出了全信息下的渐近竞争比理论极限，又设计了无需离线样本即可达到该极限的在线策略，并通过收敛速率分析和数值实验加以佐证。

Abstract: We study learning in prophet inequalities with i.i.d. rewards drawn from an exponential-type parametric family with an unknown parameter $θ$, a class that includes exponential, Pareto, and bounded-support power-family distributions. We first characterize the optimal full-information asymptotic competitive ratio for this family. In the unbounded-support case, the limit is $ {\left(θ/({θ-c_+})\right)^{c_+/θ}}/ {Γ(1-c_+/θ)},$ while in the bounded-support case, the limit is $1$. We then propose a confidence-based dynamic-programming policy for online learning. By exploiting the explicit parametric structure, the policy achieves the same optimal asymptotic competitive ratio using only online observations, without external offline samples. We further derive distribution-specific convergence rates for canonical examples. Finally, numerical experiments on synthetic instances illustrate the performance of our algorithm.

</details>


### [175] [Decision-Aligned Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26990)
*Annika Schneider,Tommy Rochussen,Joshua Stiller,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: cs.LG
