Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

</details>


### [242] [Automating Potential-based Reward Shaping with Vision Language Model Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27180)
*Henrik Müller,Daniel Kudenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-PBRS框架，利用小型视觉语言模型(VLM)的偏好反馈来学习势函数，用于基于势能的奖励塑形(PBRS)，在保持最优策略不变的前提下加速强化学习，并在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境中验证了其样本效率和抗奖励黑客能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励环境下，强化学习智能体缺乏中间反馈来指导探索和正确归因成功奖励。传统奖励塑形易导致奖励黑客（reward hacking），而PBRS虽能保证最优策略不变，却需要人工设计启发式势函数。大型VLM查询成本过高，因此探索用小型VLM替代的可行性。

Method: 提出VLM-PBRS框架：通过查询轻量级VLM获取图像对偏好标签，利用这些偏好训练一个势函数模型，将该势函数应用于基于势能的奖励塑形（PBRS）。该方法无需专家设计奖励塑形项，且保留了原始最优策略集。

Result: 在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境中的实验表明，即使小型VLM的偏好标签精度较低，仍能有效加速学习。研究揭示了VLM偏好标签准确率与样本效率提升之间的关联，并展示了该方法对奖励黑客的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VLM-PBRS是首个将VLM偏好学习应用于PBRS势函数合成的工作，提供了一种原则性、低成本的解决方案，在样本效率和抗奖励黑客方面表现优异，证明了小型VLM在奖励塑形中的实用价值。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are inherently challenging for reinforcement learning agents as they lack intermediate feedback to guide exploration and to correctly attribute the sparse success rewards to relevant parts of the trajectory. Naive reward shaping can induce reward hacking, yielding policies that exploit auxiliary signals instead of solving the intended task. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) guarantees preservation of the optimal policy set, but requires the definition of a heuristic potential function over the state space. In this work, we introduce the VLM-guided PBRS framework VLM-PBRS that learns the potential function directly from vision language model (VLM) feedback. We query a lightweight VLM to obtain preferences over image pairs and train a model of the potential function using these preferences. As this approach is based on potential-based reward shaping, it preserves the original optimal policies, and removes the need for expert-designed reward shaping terms. Because large VLMs are prohibitively expensive to invoke repeatedly during policy learning, we employ smaller, more computationally efficient VLMs. Although the resulting preference labels are less accurate, empirical evidence shows that the preference labels can still be used to accelerate learning. We validate our method empirically in the Meta-World and Franka Kitchen environments and highlight the connection between VLM preference label accuracy and sample efficiency improvements. Our contributions are threefold: (1) the first application of VLM preference-based learning to synthesize a potential function for PBRS, (2) a principled, low-cost solution that leverages small VLMs, and (3) extensive empirical demonstration of improved sample efficiency and robustness to reward hacking.

</details>


### [243] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的归因方法，基于归一化相关性度量（NRM）框架，完整分析事件型时序图神经网络（ETGNN）中所有事件相关变量的信息流，克服现有方法仅关注事件嵌入到输出路径的局限性，并通过模块化分解扩展NRM以处理复杂架构，在流行病追踪、社会动态和政治事件预测等数据集上验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETGNN解释方法仅关注事件嵌入到输出的信息流子集，忽略了通过事件诱导变量（event-induced variables）传递的重要路径，而这些变量在捕获长程时序依赖中起核心作用。ETGNN日益复杂的架构也为可解释性带来严峻挑战。

Method: 基于归一化相关性度量（NRM）框架，提出一种分析所有事件相关变量完整信息流的归因方法。具体包括：(1) 显式量化源自事件嵌入的信息流和通过事件诱导变量的信息流；(2) 确保跨层隐变量的可比较性；(3) 支持事件间交互的高阶分析。此外，通过模块化分解过程扩展NRM框架，以系统化构建复杂神经架构的相关性结构。

Result: 在两个合成数据集（流行病追踪、社会动态）和一个真实政治事件网络数据集上进行了定性和定量评估，所提方法一致优于现有解释方法，同时生成更易于人类理解的解释结果。

Conclusion: 通过完整建模ETGNN中所有事件相关变量的信息流，所提出的NRM扩展方法显著提升了可解释性，在多个数据集上验证了其有效性和人类可解释性优势。

Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [244] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇综述围绕统一的设计空间梳理了图神经网络(GNN)领域，从第一性原理推导出谱域和空域形式，并将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层级联系起来。在此基础上，对12个应用领域进行系统审查，揭示了跨领域的共性模式，并将过度平滑、过度压缩、鲁棒性等议题定位为决定GNN实际部署的关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN已从一种小众技术发展为关系型数据的默认模型类别。当前的核心问题不再是消息传递是否有效，而是图结构在何处值得其计算成本、在何处不值得。该综述旨在通过统一的方法论框架，系统性地回答这一问题，并弥合学术基准与实际部署之间的鸿沟。

Method: 围绕单一设计空间组织全领域，从共享第一性原理推导谱域和空域形式；将模型表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层级建立显式对应，明确当前架构能区分和不能区分的图结构；对12个应用领域逐一审查，分析图构建选择及其成本、主导架构家族及其原因，区分真实增益与弱基线或有利数据划分造成的人为提升；最后进行跨领域比较，识别反复出现的模式。

Result: 跨领域比较揭示了若干共性模式：异配性和图规模在几乎所有领域中都削弱了相同类型的模型；时序图普遍比静态图更难处理；在公开排行榜上领先的架构很少成为实际部署的选择。过度平滑、过度压缩、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性并非事后清单，而是决定模型能否被采纳的硬约束。

Conclusion: GNN研究的重心已从"消息传递是否有效"转向"图结构何时值得其成本"。该综述通过统一设计空间、W-L层级关联和12个领域的实证审查，指出学术基准与实际部署之间的脱节，并强调过度平滑/压缩、鲁棒性和公平性等约束是决定GNN能否落地的关键因素。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

</details>


### [245] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

</details>


### [246] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个新的语料库RSPC，包含1799条关于异地恋的Reddit帖子，由精神科医生标注了诊断类别、关系压力源触发因素和关系阶段。通过在 disorder 分类、trigger 检测和 phase 预测三个任务上评估 Transformer 和 LLM，发现不同模型家族间存在任务依赖的性能差异，并揭示了焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间的强关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP研究通常将心理健康状况建模为孤立现象，忽略了人际关系背景。作者认为，心理痛苦往往嵌入在关系动态中，因此需要构建一个将心理 distress 与其关系触发因素、时间阶段相结合的数据集，推动从以个体为中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变。

Method: 构建RSPC语料库：收集异地恋相关Reddit帖子，由精神科医生进行多标签标注（诊断类别、关系压力源触发因素、关系阶段）。在三个任务上评估七种微调的Transformer模型和五种LLM：多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测、时间阶段预测。使用Macro-F1作为主要评估指标。

Result: Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务上取得最佳Macro-F1（0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测上表现最优（Macro-F1=0.519），不同模型家族在不同任务上展现各自优势。此外，发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系情境的NLP任务建立了基准，证明了将社会和时间动态纳入心理健康建模的重要性。研究支持从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康计算方法论转变，强调人际背景在理解和预测心理痛苦中的关键作用。

Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

</details>


### [247] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个来自Instagram和Reddit的博彩广告数据集，对其进行操纵性和欺骗性广告实践的标注，并提供人工解释，以支持可解释的操纵性广告检测研究，同时分析了这些广告对心理健康的潜在影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上博彩应用的推广广告大幅增长，其中许多使用误导性说服技巧，可能引发危险行为并影响用户心理健康。然而，由于缺乏公开标注数据集，对操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告的自动检测研究十分有限。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit两个社交媒体平台收集博彩相关广告，对操纵性和欺骗性广告实践进行人工标注，不仅提供分类标签，还提供人工撰写的解释说明，记录每项标注背后的推理过程，以支持可解释检测方法的研究。此外，对博彩广告常用策略及其对心理健康的影响进行分析。

Result: 构建了一个带有分类标签和人工解释的博彩广告数据集，揭示了博彩广告中常见的操纵性策略，并分析了这些说服策略对用户心理健康的影响机制。

Conclusion: 该数据集和框架填补了操纵性博彩广告自动检测领域的数据空白，可支持开发浏览器插件以警示用户、构建自动化网络爬虫协助监管机构监控和检测在线博彩推广等实际应用。

Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

</details>


### [248] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了"更大模型带来更好预测"的主流观点，证明通过精细调优预处理超参数（如上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和数据增强），使用简单的Ridge回归即可超越Transformer、MLP、CNN等复杂基线模型，同时揭示数据本身的结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测研究持续追求更大规模的架构（从专用Transformer到通用基础模型），默认模型容量是解锁精度的关键。作者反其道而行之，认为预测性能差距可以通过低成本地优化预处理来弥合，而非扩展模型规模。

Method: 以Ridge回归（具有闭式解和可解释权重）为测试平台，在八个标准基准上对上下文长度、局部归一化策略、正则化强度和数据增强进行超参数搜索。通过分析最优超参数直接"读出"数据特性，并考察跨序列超参数共享策略（从完全共享到完全逐序列独立）。

Result: 发现三个规律：(1) 最优回溯窗口与序列强相关且往往随预测跨度非单调变化，拟合的幂律指数从ETTm2的+0.46到Exchange和Traffic的-0.19，挑战了"更长预测需要更长历史"的惯例；(2) 在上下文的可学习尾部片段上做归一化几乎总是优于全上下文归一化；(3) 同一数据集中不同序列对超参数的偏好常不一致，最优共享程度各异。所得模型在多数数据集-预测跨度组合上击败先前线性预测器，并在八个基准中的六个上超越Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。

Conclusion: 通过优化预处理而非扩大模型，可以以极低成本获得媲美甚至超越复杂模型的预测性能。优化后的超参数本身可作为数据诊断工具，揭示那些大型模型默默吸收到其学习参数中的数据结构特征。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [249] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程解集的自然度量，建立了从线性到非线性（Lipschitz/Hölder连续）的各类常微分方程的可识别性边界，并基于度量熵估计推导了样本复杂度界限，量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需的观测数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前从观测数据中学习控制方程的研究缺乏理论基础：一方面，真值ODE在多次解观测下被唯一且稳定识别的理论条件尚未建立；另一方面，文献中不存在对此类学习任务样本复杂度的定量分析。

Method: 作者引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，该度量刻画了两方程在所有容许初始条件下最坏情况的分离程度，编码了识别问题的极小极大结构。基于此度量，作者为从线性ODE到具有Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场的非线性方程类建立了可识别性界限，并推导了相关ODE类的度量熵估计与样本复杂度界限。

Result: 作者建立了广泛结构方程类的可识别性界限，精确刻画了两个不同方程何时可从解数据中区分。进一步导出了ODE类的度量熵估计，并给出了样本复杂度界限，量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需解观测的数量。

Conclusion: Hausdorff距离是分析ODE识别问题的恰当理论工具。本文填补了控制方程学习在可识别性理论与样本复杂度定量分析方面的理论空白，为科学机器学习中方程发现任务提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [250] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种RLAIF框架，用于生成可迁移的求职搜索查询词。研究发现，在无critic的优化器中，性能主要取决于鲁棒的奖励塑形而非算法选择；GRPO对虚假奖励信号异常敏感，而引入基于规则的奖励底线可有效缓解奖励黑客行为，带来+0.147的质量提升。训练时奖励模型将性能增益夸大了2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职搜索平台依赖低带宽查询接口，无法捕捉候选人画像的高维复杂性。现有方案难以生成既能剥离求职者个人标识又能保留通用资质的"可迁移"查询词，且该任务的对抗性奖励曲面极易被策略优化所利用，产生退化性的逐字复制行为。

Method: 提出端到端RLAIF框架生成可迁移求职查询词。系统性地对比了多种无critic优化器（RLOO、REINFORCE++、GRPO）在该对抗性奖励环境中的表现，并引入确定性、基于规则的奖励底线（reward floor）来修正逐字复制行为所获得的虚假奖励，从而抑制奖励黑客现象。

Result: （1）无critic优化器中，鲁棒的奖励塑形对性能起主导作用，算法选择影响甚微；（2）GRPO的组相对优势归一化对虚假奖励信号异常敏感，最易被利用；（3）引入基于规则的奖励底线后，跨家族评估裁判上质量提升+0.147；（4）训练时奖励模型将性能增益夸大了2.4倍，证实训练成功依赖于奖励塑形纪律。

Conclusion: 在高度对抗性的奖励环境中，奖励塑形（reward shaping）的鲁棒性远比优化器选择更为关键。GRPO的组相对优势归一化机制使其成为奖励黑客的薄弱环节，但可通过确定性规则奖励底线有效补救。训练时奖励模型显著高估实际增益，需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [251] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多保真迁移学习框架，结合轻量级一维时域谱元法仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习与前馈神经网络，利用少量实验数据实现板状结构损伤的精确定位与尺寸量化，定位R²>0.93，尺寸量化R²>0.99。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于导波的结构健康监测中，深度学习模型的实际部署受限于标注实验数据稀缺以及大规模高保真仿真数据计算成本过高的问题。

Method: 采用计算高效的一维时域谱元法生成大量低计算成本的合成数据进行预训练；通过卷积自编码器（CAE）进行深度特征学习，提取导波信号的鲁棒特征表示；再借助迁移学习，利用少量标注实验数据将模型从仿真域适配至实验域；最终由前馈神经网络输出损伤位置与尺寸预测。

Result: CAE迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于CNN对应方案；定位R²>0.93，尺寸量化R²>0.99；对预训练和微调阶段未见的损伤场景也展现出高预测精度和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出框架是面向实际导波结构健康监测应用的一种精准、计算高效且实用的解决方案，有效克服了标注数据不足与仿真计算成本高的双重挑战。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [252] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了梯度均衡（GEQ）与Blackwell可逼近性在算法意义上是等价的，从而将GEQ纳入更广泛的在线学习框架体系中，与遗憾最小化、校准等问题建立起等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEQ作为近期提出的在线优化框架，与遗憾最小化等已知框架的关系尚不清晰。先前工作仅表明GEQ误差与遗憾是不可比较的目标，作者旨在精确定位GEQ在在线学习领域中的位置。

Method: 作者建立了GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性之间的双向黑盒归约：一方面从逼近性问题到GEQ oracle的归约，另一方面反之亦然。归约过程是高效的，且能保持oracle误差率无渐近损失。通过这些归约，结合已知的逼近性、遗憾最小化和校准之间的等价关系，将等价性传递到GEQ。

Result: 1. GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性在算法上等价；2. 因此GEQ也与遗憾最小化和校准等价；3. 归约可用于将乐观性和强自适应性等精细保证从遗憾最小化迁移到GEQ；4. 给出了GEQ的充要条件，并建立了无约束与有约束决策集下不同GEQ概念之间的归约。

Conclusion: GEQ并非孤立的新框架，而是与Blackwell可逼近性、遗憾最小化及校准处于同一等价类中。这一发现不仅统一了在线学习的理论图景，也为GEQ提供了从其他框架迁移算法与理论保证的桥梁。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [253] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种可与Top-k SAE架构兼容的稀疏正则化器（ℓ₁惩罚和ℓ₁/ℓ₂比率惩罚），作用于Top-k选择前的激活上，在不损失重建质量的前提下持续提升特征的单语义性，证明硬架构稀疏与软正则化稀疏是互补而非互斥的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k SAE虽已成为标准方案，但存在两个局限：(1) 稀疏预算k固定，无法根据输入复杂度自适应调整；(2) 模型倾向于过拟合训练时的k值，导致推理时k变化时重建质量下降。此前由于Top-k SAE设计初衷就是避免ℓ₁惩罚的缺陷，因此从未与显式稀疏正则化器结合。作者旨在探索两者是否可以互补。

Method: 提出两种作用于Top-k选择前的稀疏正则化器：(1) ℓ₁惩罚：对未被选中的单元施加ℓ₁惩罚；(2) 尺度不变的ℓ₁/ℓ₂比率惩罚：将编码集中在更少的有效单元上。两种惩罚仅施加于批次活跃单元（batch-active units），即在一个批次内至少被Top-k选中一次的隐变量。在2个数据集、3个视觉基础模型、不同k值下验证。

Result: 两种正则化器均在不牺牲重建质量的前提下持续提升单语义性（monosemanticity）。ℓ₁/ℓ₂惩罚进一步将信息集中到更少的隐变量中，使重建对推理时k的选择更加鲁棒，并改善了小预算下的线性探测性能。

Conclusion: 核心发现：硬架构稀疏（Top-k选择）与软稀疏正则化（ℓ₁和ℓ₁/ℓ₂惩罚）是互补关系而非互斥关系，两者结合可同时获得更稀疏、更单语义、更鲁棒的特征表示。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [254] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型的幻觉源于状态-动作空间的低覆盖率区域，通过三个检测信号（感知、动作边缘化、场景偏离）可精准预测失败点，并利用覆盖率感知采样和数据高效微调（仅需50条轨迹即可适应新环境）来缓解幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式世界模型虽能生成逼真的动作可控未来帧，但常出现"幻觉"——画面流畅却偏离真实动力学。作者假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，且轻量级数据中心信号既能检测幻觉也能指导缓解。

Method: 1. 构建MMBench2数据集（427小时、210任务），含真值动作、奖励和实时模拟器；2. 训练350M参数世界模型；3. 识别三种幻觉模式（感知幻觉、动作边缘化幻觉、场景偏离幻觉），对应pipeline不同阶段；4. 开发三个预测信号定位模型失败点；5. 训练阶段使用覆盖率感知采样填补覆盖缺口，在线阶段将幻觉预测器作为好奇心奖励驱动目标数据收集；6. 仅用50条真实环境轨迹即可微调适应全新环境。

Result: 三种幻觉模式被明确区分并与pipeline各阶段锚定；幻觉预测信号能准确预测模型失败位置；覆盖率感知采样和基于幻觉预测器的好奇心奖励机制有效缩小覆盖缺口；数据高效微调方法仅需50条轨迹即可将预训练世界模型适配到完全未见过的环境。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖问题，用于检测幻觉的同一套信号也可用于缓解幻觉。这一发现为构建更可靠的世界模型提供了数据中心的新范式。

Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

</details>


### [255] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ENS 将 PDE 残差场作为网络的直接输入，让模型学习迭代修正自身的预测误差，而非将其作为优化目标，从而在多个 PDE 系统中实现最高精度，且无需混合方法的昂贵计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经验证模型（surrogate models）无法纠错且外推能力差；混合方法虽引入物理约束但继承经典优化器的计算代价与不稳定性。更重要的是，理论和实验表明在病态系统中，最小化 PDE 残差与重建精度并不一致，因此需要一种新的范式。

Method: 提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS）：将 PDE 残差场作为网络每一步迭代的直接输入，使网络能够读取自身误差的空间结构，学习一个迭代更新策略来逐步修正预测，而非将残差最小化作为优化目标。

Result: 在四类 PDE 族上，ENS 在绝大多数设置下达到最高预测精度，湍流 Kolmogorov 流上提升达 10 倍；其学习到的修正策略在分布偏移（零样本参数变化、跨方程迁移）下泛化良好，且在病态条件下相对优势最大。

Conclusion: ENS 提供了一种比残差最小化混合方法更可靠、更高效的物理约束策略，避免了将 PDE 残差作为优化代理目标所带来的不可靠性，在病态系统中尤为显著。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>


### [256] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），一种摒弃传统流模型范式的新型自回归建模框架。ArBG绕开了流模型的拓扑限制，支持推理时序列干预，并可借助大语言模型架构实现高效扩展。在肽系统基准测试中，ArBG全面超越流式玻尔兹曼生成器，其132M参数的可迁移模型Robin在8残基系统上将零样本能量误差降低了超60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有玻尔兹曼生成器（BGs）主要依赖归一化流（NFs），存在两个核心瓶颈：离散时间流因严格可逆性约束导致表达能力受限，连续时间流则面临似然计算成本高昂的问题。因此需要一种摆脱流模型范式的新框架来同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），采用自回归建模替代基于流的范式。该框架将分子构象的联合分布分解为条件分布的乘积，天然避免拓扑约束；支持推理阶段的序列条件干预；并借鉴大语言模型的高效架构设计来提升可扩展性。此外，构建了132M参数的Robin可迁移模型，在ArBG框架下训练。

Result: ArBG在所有基准测试上均显著优于基于流的模型，尤其在10残基Chignolin等较大肽系统中优势突出。Robin模型刷新了先前最优水平，在8残基系统上将零样本能量误差E-W₂降低超过60%。

Conclusion: 自回归建模是玻尔兹曼生成器的有效替代范式，能同时克服离散和连续时间流的表达性与计算成本问题，并在分子构象采样任务中展现出更强的性能与可迁移性。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [257] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiVER 提出了一种无需真实答案的强化学习框架，通过排名诱导的验证机制和校准的奖励塑形，利用确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督，在基于评分的优化任务上训练LLMs。该方法在提升启发式优化能力的同时，意外地迁移到了精确求解基准上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RLVR依赖真实答案来分配奖励，限制了其在无真实解的任务（如启发式搜索、代码优化等基于评分的任务）上的应用。作者旨在突破这一限制，让LLM在不依赖ground-truth的条件下也能通过RL进行有效训练。

Method: 提出RiVER框架：(1) 使用确定性执行反馈（如测试用例通过率、启发式得分）作为连续值奖励信号；(2) 识别并解决两大挑战：尺度主导（不同实例间未校准的分数量级扭曲策略更新）和频率主导（重复采样的次优解淹没稀有但优秀的解）；(3) 采用校准奖励塑形，通过实例级比较和强调排名靠前的解法来缓解上述问题，同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈。

Result: 在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414在ALE-Bench评分排名上分别提升8.9%和9.4%。更关键的是，尽管仅在基于评分的任务上训练（无真实解），RiVER在LiveCodeBench和USACO等精确求解基准上也分别带来2.4%和3.5%的绝对平均提升。而使用原始执行分数训练的基线虽能提升ALE评分，却无法迁移到精确求解基准。

Conclusion: 基于评分的优化任务配合适当的奖励校准，可以作为无需真实答案的通用编码能力训练环境。这暗示了RL训练范式的扩展潜力——从需要答案监督的任务扩展到仅需相对优劣信号的优化任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Explainable Outlier Detection for Interval-valued Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26307)
*Catarina P. Loureiro,M. Rosário Oliveira,Paula Brito,Lina Oliveira*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Shapley值的区间值数据异常检测可解释性方法，通过推导平方稳健区间马氏距离的Shapley值闭式解，将异常值分解为中心、范围和交叉项的贡献，并扩展到Shapley交互指数以捕捉变量间的配对交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性在异常检测中日益重要，但对于区间值这类复杂数据结构，现有研究几乎空白。亟需一种能够精细解释区间值观测异常成因的方法。

Method: 基于区间最小协方差行列式(IMCD)估计器的异常检测框架，推导平方稳健区间马氏距离的Shapley值闭式表达式，将异常度分解为中心贡献、范围贡献及交叉项贡献；进一步引入Shapley交互指数捕捉变量间的配对交互效应，并关联到单元异常(cellwise outliers)的识别。

Result: 所提方法能够实现对区间值异常观测的细粒度解释，明确各变量的中心、范围及交叉项对异常度的贡献，并能识别在多元层面不显著但在单变量层面存在的单元异常。通过两个真实数据集验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该框架为区间值数据的异常检测提供了系统性的可解释性方案，Shapley值及其交互扩展不仅能量化变量贡献，还能揭示变量间的非加性交互效应，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: Explainability is increasingly recognized as a key aspect of outlier detection. However, for complex data structures such as interval-valued data, it remains largely unexplored. Building on an outlier detection framework based on the Interval Minimum Covariance Determinant estimator, we propose a novel approach to explain the outlyingness of interval-valued observations using the concept of the Shapley value. We derive a closed-form expression for the Shapley value of the squared robust Interval-Mahalanobis distance, enabling efficient computation of variable contributions. This formulation allows for a fine-grained interpretation of outliers, providing a detailed decomposition into contributions from centers, ranges, and cross-terms of the interval-valued observations. Moreover, the Shapley value is closely connected to the concept of cellwise outliers, as it can help identify variable-specific outliers that may not be evident at multivariate level. We further extend the framework through the Shapley interaction index to capture pairwise variable interactions driving atypical behavior. The practical utility of the proposed approach is illustrated through two real-world datasets.

</details>


### [259] [Variance Deltas for Visualizing and Explaining Posterior Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26309)
*Collin Cademartori*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出"方差增量"交互式可视化系统，从贝叶斯后验分布中自动发现并组织缺失信息，帮助研究者理解哪些未观测变量对目标参数的不确定性贡献最大，并通过模拟和真实民调数据验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在观测性研究中，数据生成过程和样本量不受控制，无法先验保证感兴趣的参数能以足够精度估计。当数据本身不足以确定目标量时，如何从后验分布中发现"缺失信息"——即那些如果被观测到就能显著降低不确定性的未观测变量——成为一个关键问题。

Method: 提出一种名为"方差增量"(variance deltas)的交互式可视化系统，以后验分布为基础，将缺失信息表示为未观测模型量的子集，并按照每个子集对目标量不确定性的解释程度组织成树状结构。系统包含两个核心功能：(1) 从最小用户输入自动构建候选子集；(2) 实现子集分割与合并的交互操作，支持高效探索。提供了配套软件包。

Result: 通过两个案例验证系统有效性：(1) 模拟因果推断问题中治疗效应参数的不确定性解释；(2) 基于真实民调数据的预测模型中人口比例参数的不确定性解释，该模型包含多种偏差来源。系统成功发现了非显而易见的、有意义的解释。

Conclusion: 方差增量系统为贝叶斯后验分析中的不确定性溯源提供了一种有效的交互式工具，能够在观测性研究场景下帮助研究者识别对估计精度最关键但尚未观测的信息，从而为后续数据收集或模型改进提供指导。

Abstract: In observational settings, where the data generating process and possibly the sample size are not controlled, it is typically impossible to guarantee a priori that quantities of interest will be estimated with sufficient precision. However, even when the data do not determine the quantities of interest, they may still allow determination of what is missing -- unobserved information which, if observed, would meaningfully reduce uncertainty. We propose an interactive visualization system, termed variance deltas, to enable the discovery of such missing information from a Bayesian posterior distribution. This system, which we provide as a software package, represents missing information as subsets of unobserved model quantities, organized into a tree based on how well each subset explains uncertainty about the quantity of interest. This system both automates the construction of candidate subsets from minimal user input and implements interactive operations for the division and combination of subsets, allowing the efficient discovery of interesting and useful explanations. We demonstrate this system by using it to discover nonobvious explanations of uncertainty for (1) a treatment effect parameter in a simulated causal inference problem and (2) a population proportion in a forecasting model of real polling data with many sources of bias.

</details>


### [260] [A unified approach to outlier identification for mixed-type data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26324)
*Efthymios Costa,Christian Hennig*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种面向混合类型数据（连续变量与有序变量）的异常值识别方法，基于多元高斯参考分布，对有序变量引入潜高斯假设，采用稳健最小协方差行列式（MCD）估计量进行参数估计，并通过模拟和Airbnb实际数据验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常值检测方法多局限于纯连续或纯分类数据，难以有效处理同时包含连续变量和有序变量的混合类型数据集。本文旨在填补这一方法学空白，提供一种能够在混合数据中稳健识别异常值的统一框架。

Method: 以多元高斯分布作为非异常值的参考分布，对有序变量假设其背后存在未观测的潜高斯变量。采用稳健的MCD（Minimum Covariance Determinant）估计量估计非异常值群体的多元高斯参数，并将该方法扩展以处理有序变量中潜高斯信息不可直接观测的问题。同时给出了一个破坏点定理（breakdown theorem），证明替换观测值不会阻止识别足够极端的异常值。

Result: 合成数据模拟实验表明，该方法在多种污染类型下均能实现高检测率和低误报率。Airbnb房源数据的实际应用进一步验证了方法的实用价值，成功识别出包含连续和有序属性异常的房源记录。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效且稳健地识别混合类型数据中的异常值，兼具理论保证（破坏点定理）和实际可操作性，为混合数据异常检测提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: We present an outlier identification method for mixed type data sets comprising continuous and ordinal variables. We define outliers based on using a multivariate Gaussian distribution as reference distribution for non-outliers, with a latent Gaussian assumed for ordinal variables. The proposed algorithm is based on the robust Minimum Covariance Determinant estimator for estimating the parameters of the multivariate Gaussian for the non-outliers. This is extended to account for the fact that the full Gaussian information underlying the ordinal variables is not observed. A breakdown theorem shows that replacing observations will noty stop extreme enough outliers from being identified. The effectiveness of our approach is demonstrated via simulations on synthetic data with various types of contamination, achieving high detection and low false positive rates. Practical relevance is illustrated through an application to Airbnb listing data containing both continuous and ordinal attributes.

</details>


### [261] [Incomplete Matrix Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26325)
*Khaled Fouda,Aurélie Labbe,Karim Oualkacha*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出Incomplete Matrix Regression (IMR)，一个将行/列协变量信息与矩阵补全相结合的无分布惩罚回归框架，通过Lasso惩罚建模协变量效应、低秩分解捕捉残差结构，并利用Ridge惩罚融入空间/时间等相似性核，在保证预测精度的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多应用（如推荐系统、城市出行）中，待补全的矩阵往往伴随行/列上的辅助协变量，且存在行列间的依赖性，但现有矩阵补全方法未能充分利用这些辅助信息。因此需要一种统一框架，将协变量信息与低秩结构有机结合，同时保持计算可扩展性。

Method: 提出IMR框架，将目标矩阵建模为截距项、Lasso正则化的协变量效应、以及低秩隐成分之和；通过Ridge型惩罚将已知时空核等相似性结构纳入隐因子；估计采用可扩展的交替最小二乘算法，模块化设计允许灵活增减模型组件而无需重新推导更新公式。

Result: 建立了Lasso估计量和矩阵补全估计量的非渐近误差界，与各自文献中的标准速率一致。模拟研究和两个真实数据应用表明，IMR以远低于复杂方法的计算成本达到具有竞争力的预测精度。方法已在R包IMR中实现。

Conclusion: IMR成功地将协变量信息与低秩矩阵补全统一在一个分布自由的惩罚回归框架中，在统计精度与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Matrix completion seeks to recover a low-rank matrix from a sparse and noisy subset of its entries. In many applications, such as recommendation systems and urban mobility, the observed matrix is accompanied by auxiliary covariates on its rows and columns and exhibits dependence across them. We propose Incomplete Matrix Regression (IMR), a distribution-free penalized regression framework that integrates such information into matrix completion. The target matrix is modeled as the sum of intercepts, covariate effects regularized by a Lasso penalty, and a low-rank latent component that captures structure unexplained by the covariates. Known similarity structures, such as spatial and temporal kernels, are incorporated through ridge-type penalties on the latent factors. For estimation, we provide a scalable alternating least-squares algorithm whose modular form allows us to include or exclude individual model components without rederiving the updates. We establish non-asymptotic error bounds for both the Lasso and matrix completion estimators that are consistent with standard rates in their respective literature. Through simulation studies and two real-data applications, we demonstrate that the proposed method attains predictive accuracy competitive with more complex methods at a small fraction of their computational cost. The methodology is implemented in the R package IMR.

</details>


### [262] [Robust Estimation of Polychoric Correlation for Complex Survey Designs Using Minimum Divergence Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26351)
*Siqi Wei,David Kepplinger,Anand N. Vidyashankar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对调查数据中多项相关（polychoric correlation）估计易受污染影响的问题，本文提出了基于Hellinger距离和负指数差异两种最小散度准则的稳健估计量，结合Horvitz-Thompson调整处理复杂抽样设计，并通过惩罚方法优化参数估计，为不同污染模式下的方法选择提供了实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准极大似然估计在估计多项相关时对所有观测赋予等权，对调查数据中的反应误差、访员效应、粗心作答等污染高度敏感。现有方法缺乏在复杂抽样设计下对多项相关进行稳健估计的手段，亟需开发能结合抽样权重且抵抗污染的估计方法。

Method: 提出两类基于最小散度准则的稳健估计量：(1) Hellinger距离（HD）方法，含Ridge和Lasso惩罚变体，对冗余参数正则化而保留相关系数不惩罚；(2) 负指数差异（NED）方法。两者均通过Horvitz-Thompson调整的单元格频率纳入抽样权重。建立了HD估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，推导了反映抽样设计的夹层协方差。通过Poisson比例抽样下的模拟，考察三种污染几何形态（一致上尾、一致下尾、不一致混合角点）与标准/非标准潜在边际分布的交叉组合。

Result: 两类估计量具有互补优势：惩罚HD方法在一致性污染（concordant contamination）下均方误差最低；NED方法在不一致污染（discordant contamination）及复合误设-污染效应下表现最优。HD的影响函数有限但非一致有界，反映了Hellinger距离对稀疏单元格的敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究者应根据调查实践中预期的污染模式选择合适方法：预期一致性污染时优先考虑惩罚HD方法，预期不一致污染或复合误设时优先考虑NED方法。该框架为复杂抽样调查中多项相关的稳健推断提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Standard maximum likelihood estimation of polychoric correlations is highly sensitive to contamination in survey data, including response errors, interviewer effects, and careless responding, yet assigns equal weight to all observations regardless of data quality. We develop robust estimators for polychoric correlation under complex survey designs based on two minimum divergence criteria -- Hellinger distance (HD) and negative exponential disparity (NED) -- incorporating survey weights through Horvitz--Thompson adjusted cell frequencies. For HD, we propose penalized Ridge and Lasso variants that regularize nuisance parameters while leaving the correlation unpenalized, and establish consistency and asymptotic normality with a sandwich covariance reflecting the sampling design. The influence function is finite but not uniformly bounded, reflecting Hellinger's sensitivity to sparse cells. Simulations under Poisson proportional-to-size sampling examine three contamination geometries -- concordant upper, concordant lower, and discordant mixed corner -- crossed with standard and non-standard latent marginals. The two estimator classes offer complementary advantages: penalized HD methods achieve the lowest mean squared error under concordant contamination, while NED performs best under discordant contamination and under compound misspecification--contamination effects. We provide practical guidelines for method selection based on anticipated contamination patterns in survey practice.

</details>


### [263] [Learning rate selection via weighted Fisher divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26478)
*Takahiro Onizuka*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对广义贝叶斯推断中的校准问题，提出了一种基于加权Fisher散度的学习率选择方法，得到闭式解，并通过数值实验和真实数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义贝叶斯方法引入基于损失的似然和可调学习率，具有高度灵活性，但在模型误设定下存在严重的校准问题：传统贝叶斯信息等式失效，未校准的Gibbs后验给出的可信集缺乏正确的频率学派覆盖概率解释。因此需要一种系统的方法来选择合适的学习率来校准广义后验。

Method: 作者引入加权Fisher散度（weighted Fisher divergence），度量广义后验的渐近分布与具有夹心型方差（sandwich-type variance）的正态分布之间的距离。通过最小化该散度，推导出学习率的闭式表达式。该学习率包含了经典的Fisher信息匹配学习率作为特例，且在一类重要的特殊情形下不大于后者。

Result: 所提出的学习率选择方法具有闭式解，包含了Fisher信息匹配学习率作为特例，且在某些重要情形下给出更小的学习率值。数值算例和真实数据分析展示了该方法在校准广义后验方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过加权Fisher散度框架选择学习率，可以有效解决广义贝叶斯推断在模型误设定下的校准问题，为构造具有正确不确定性解释的可信集提供了理论依据和实用工具。

Abstract: The general Bayesian approach provides a flexible modeling framework by introducing a loss-based likelihood. A general posterior has a learning rate, which controls the relative weight of the loss-based likelihood and the prior. The flexibility of general Bayesian inference comes with an important calibration problem, especially under model misspecification. In such cases, the conventional Bayesian information identity fails, and credible sets derived from an uncalibrated Gibbs posterior need not have the desired uncertainty interpretation. This paper aims to select the learning rate used to calibrate a general posterior. By introducing the weighted Fisher divergence between the asymptotic distribution of the general posterior and a normal distribution with sandwich-type variance, we provide a closed-form expression for the selected learning rate. The selected learning rate includes the Fisher information matching learning rate as a special case and is no larger than it in an important special case. Numerical examples and a real data analysis demonstrate the usefulness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [264] [$λ$-PSD: Scalable Approximate SNR-Optimised Polynomial Stein Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26621)
*Minh-Long Nguyen,Thanh-Long Vu,Christopher Drovandi,Leah F. South,Trung-Tin Nguyen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 多项式Stein差异(PSD)存在信噪比随多项式次数指数衰减的问题。本文将此问题形式化为SNR²最大化，提出λ-PSD——一种在低维子空间中通过协方差感知重加权来稳定SNR²的方法，在保持线性时间复杂度的同时显著提升检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PSD方法虽然计算可扩展，但其统计特性不明。作者发现提高多项式次数反而导致信噪比(SNR²)指数级衰减，这构成一个严重的失效模式，需要从SNR优化的角度重新设计Stein差异。

Method: 将Stein差异构造重新表述为显式的SNR²最大化问题，转化为Stein特征上的Rayleigh商形式。在此基础上提出λ-PSD，一种在低维子空间中定义的近似可扩展协方差感知重加权方案，通过协方差结构信息来控制方差并稳定SNR²。

Result: 理论证明：在高斯设定下，λ-PSD避免了SNR²的指数崩塌，实现了稳定的SNR²。实验表明：λ-PSD在保持样本数线性时间复杂度的前提下，大幅提升了检验功效。

Conclusion: SNR感知设计对可扩展Stein差异方法至关重要。λ-PSD通过协方差感知重加权有效解决了PSD的信噪比退化问题，为大规模拟合优度检验提供了理论和实践上都更优的方案。

Abstract: Polynomial Stein discrepancies (PSD) provide a scalable alternative to kernel Stein methods for measuring sample quality and goodness-of-fit testing, but their statistical properties remain poorly understood. We show that increasing polynomial degree primarily amplifies signal without adequately controlling variance, rather than directly optimising the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Under suitable assumptions, this might lead to a failure mode in which the $\text{SNR}^2$ can provably decay exponentially with polynomial degree. Motivated by this observation, we reformulate Stein discrepancy construction as an explicit $\text{SNR}^2$ maximisation problem, yielding a Rayleigh quotient over Stein features. This perspective motivates $λ$-PSD, an approximate scalable covariance-aware reweighting scheme defined in a low-dimensional subspace. Under Gaussian settings, we show that $λ$-PSD avoids the exponential $\text{SNR}^2$ collapse and achieves a stable $\text{SNR}^2$. Empirically, $λ$-PSD substantially improves test power while retaining linear-time complexity in the number of samples, highlighting the importance of SNR-aware design for scalable Stein discrepancies.

</details>


### [265] [A Unified Three-Stage Weighting Framework for Causal Inference and Mediation Analysis under Case-Control Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26543)
*Tarikul Islam,Mahbub A. H. M. Latif*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的三阶段加权(3S-weighting)框架，用于病例-对照研究中的因果推断和因果中介分析。该框架首先通过密度比学习和标签偏移校正估计未知的总体结局患病率，然后利用基于患病率的设计权重重建目标总体分布，最后在边际结构模型框架内应用稳定的因果和中介权重，以估计总效应和路径特异性因果效应（纯直接效应、纯间接效应和交互效应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病例-对照研究因其在罕见结局或前瞻性随访不可行时的高效性而被广泛使用，但回顾性、结局依赖的抽样方式扭曲了总体结局分布，给因果推断带来根本性挑战。传统分析方法忽视回顾性抽样机制会导致总效应和中介效应估计产生严重偏倚，亟需一套能够纠正这种抽样偏倚的统一因果推断框架。

Method: 提出三阶段加权(3S-weighting)框架：第一阶段，利用密度比学习和标签偏移校正，结合外部协变量信息，估计未知的总体结局患病率；第二阶段，使用基于患病率的设计权重将回顾性样本重建为目标总体分布；第三阶段，在边际结构模型框架中应用稳定的因果权重和中介权重，估计总效应及路径特异性效应（纯直接效应、纯间接效应和交互效应）。通过模拟研究和NHANES实际数据分析验证方法性能。

Result: 模拟研究表明，忽视回顾性抽样的传统分析方法在总效应和中介效应估计中均产生显著偏倚；相比之下，所提方法在多种抽样场景下均能一致地恢复目标总体的因果参数。NHANES数据的应用进一步展示了该框架的实际可操作性与应用价值。

Conclusion: 3S-weighting框架为病例-对照研究中的因果推断和因果中介分析提供了统一且有效的解决方案，能够纠正回顾性抽样带来的偏倚，使研究者能够在结局依赖的抽样设计下获得可靠的总效应和路径特异性因果效应估计。

Abstract: Case-control studies are widely used in epidemiology and biomedical research because they provide substantial efficiency gains when outcomes are rare or prospective follow-up is impractical. However, retrospective outcome-dependent sampling distorts the population outcome distribution, creating fundamental challenges for causal inference. We propose a unified three-stage weighting (3S-weighting) framework for causal inference and causal mediation analysis from case--control studies. The proposed approach first estimates the unknown population outcome prevalence using density-ratio learning and label-shift correction combined with externally available covariate information. Next, prevalence-based design weights are used to reconstruct the target population distribution from the retrospective sample. Finally, stabilized causal and mediation weights are applied within a marginal structural modeling framework to estimate total and pathway-specific causal effects, including the pure direct effect, pure indirect effect, and interaction effect. Simulation studies demonstrate that conventional analyses that ignore retrospective sampling can produce substantial bias in both total and mediation effect estimates, whereas the proposed approach consistently recovers the target population causal parameters across a range of sampling scenarios. An application of data from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey further illustrates the practical implementation and utility of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [266] [Fast Robust Regression via Orthogonal Block Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26611)
*Anthony Christidis,Matias Salibian-Barrera*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出ROBU（Robust Orthogonal Block Updates）算法，一种基于块坐标下降的高可扩展稳健回归方法。通过将高维解释变量分解为低维块，大幅减少寻找干净初始点所需的随机子样本数量，解决了传统MM估计器在面对中高维数据时组合爆炸的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳健回归中MM估计器的计算依赖于优良初始点。常用的随机子抽样方法为获得一个无异常值的干净子集，所需子样本数量随预测变量数呈指数增长，在中高维场景下不可行；近期提出的非随机替代策略在大样本、多参数情况下同样扩展性不佳。

Method: 提出ROBU算法，采用块坐标下降思想：将解释变量划分为若干低维正交块，在每个低维块上分别进行稳健回归（只需少量子样本即可高概率获得干净初始点），然后交替更新各块系数直至收敛。利用低维块的组合优势克服维度灾难。

Result: 通过大量仿真实验和蛋白质组学数据应用验证了ROBU的稳健性。结果表明，ROBU计算的估计量在抗异常值和抗高杠杆点方面的表现与现有算法相当或更优，且计算可扩展性显著提升。

Conclusion: ROBU算法成功解决了MM估计器在高维场景下的可扩展性瓶颈，在保持优良稳健性质的同时实现了高效计算，为大规模数据集的稳健回归分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Robust regression methods, particularly MM-estimators, are essential for analyzing datasets where heavy-tailed noise or high-leverage outliers may be present. Algorithms to compute these estimators are iterative and rely on having a good initial point. A widely-used probabilistic approach to obtaining an initial regression estimator that is not affected by outliers consists of fitting linear regression models to many random subsets of the training data. Unfortunately, the number of subsets that need to be considered in order to have a high probability of finding one that is clean of outliers grows exponentially with the number of predictors. This renders the approach unfeasible for models with a moderate to large numbers of variables. Alternative non-stochastic strategies that have been proposed recently also fail to scale well when the number of parameters and observations are large. To overcome this problem, we propose a highly scalable algorithm based on block-coordinate descent. Our Robust Orthogonal Block Updates (ROBU) algorithm uses lower-dimensional blocks of explanatory variables, which require much fewer sub-samples to find a good initial point with high probability. Extensive simulations and a proteogenomics data application illustrate the robustness properties of the estimators computed with ROBU and demonstrate that they compare favourably to those calculated with existing algorithms.

</details>


### [267] [Doubly Robust Quadratic Inference Functions for Causal Inference in Cluster Randomized Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26630)
*Hengshi Yu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双重稳健的二次推断函数（DR-QIF）估计量，结合倾向得分与结局回归模型构建双重稳健伪结局，嵌入QIF扩展得分方程，用于处理集群随机试验中因协变量不平衡导致的混杂问题。相比DR-GEE，DR-QIF在工作相关结构误设时具有更高的渐近效率，且在纵向CRT中当时间相关性较强时效率增益可达3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QIF方法未考虑集群随机试验（CRT）中治疗组间协变量不平衡带来的混杂问题，这在观察性CRT或需进行预后协变量调整的CRT中普遍存在。研究者希望填补这一方法学空白，开发既保留QIF稳健性与高效性、又能实现双重稳健保护的估计方法。

Method: 提出DR-QIF估计量：首先利用倾向得分模型和结局回归模型分别构建双重稳健伪结局，然后将这些伪结局嵌入QIF的扩展得分方程中进行估计。通过解析推导，刻画了DR-QIF相对于DR-GEE的渐近效率增益，并证明在横截面CRT中两者代数等价；效率增益仅在纵向CRT中当工作相关结构误设时显现。

Result: DR-QIF在倾向得分模型或结局回归模型其一正确设定时即可保持一致性。对于横截面CRT，DR-QIF与DR-GEE代数等价；在纵向CRT中，当N=120、T=8次重复测量且时间相关性较强时，效率增益达3.5%。蒙特卡洛模拟验证了有限样本性质，WASH Benefits Kenya CRT数据被用于方法实证说明。

Conclusion: DR-QIF为集群随机试验中的边际回归提供了一种兼具双重稳健性与更高渐近效率的替代方案，尤其适用于工作相关结构可能误设的纵向CRT场景。

Abstract: Quadratic inference functions (QIF) provide a robust and efficient alternative to generalized estimating equations (GEE) for marginal regression with correlated data, particularly in cluster randomized trials (CRTs). However, existing QIF methodology does not account for confounding due to covariate imbalance between treatment arms, a common concern in observational CRTs or CRTs with prognostic covariate adjustment. We propose a doubly robust QIF (DR-QIF) estimator that combines doubly robust pseudo-outcomes, constructed from propensity score and outcome regression models, with the QIF extended score equations. The DR-QIF estimator is consistent for the average treatment effect when either the propensity score model or the outcome regression model is correctly specified, but not necessarily both. We show that DR-QIF is more efficient than doubly robust GEE (DR-GEE) when the working correlation structure is misspecified, and we characterize the asymptotic efficiency gain analytically. For cross-sectional CRTs the two estimators are algebraically identical; efficiency gains emerge in longitudinal CRTs with strong temporal correlation, reaching 3.5% at N=120 and T=8 repeated measures. Finite-sample properties are evaluated via Monte Carlo simulation, and the method is illustrated using data from the WASH Benefits Kenya cluster randomized trial.

</details>


### [268] [Multivariable Mendelian randomization with weak instruments: a comparison of Bayesian and frequentist methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26638)
*Andrew J. Grant,Ashish Patel,Stephen Burgess*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了MVMR-Pony，一种基于贝叶斯框架的多变量孟德尔随机化方法，用于缓解条件弱工具变量导致的偏倚。模拟研究表明，该方法在偏倚、覆盖率、I类错误率和统计功效方面均优于现有的频率学方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多变量孟德尔随机化中，即使遗传工具变量与某个暴露强相关，在条件于其他暴露后可能变为弱工具变量，这导致弱工具变量偏倚问题比单变量情形更难处理。现有缓解多变量弱工具变量偏倚的方法较少，亟需新的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出MVMR-Pony方法，采用贝叶斯框架在多变量孟德尔随机化中缓解弱工具变量偏倚，并与现有频率学方法进行系统比较。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在因遗传效应相关、测量误差和中介效应导致弱工具变量偏倚的多种情境下，MVMR-Pony方法在偏倚、覆盖率、I类错误率和统计功效方面均优于频率学方法。

Conclusion: MVMR-Pony作为一种贝叶斯多变量孟德尔随机化方法，能有效缓解条件弱工具变量带来的偏倚，相较于现有频率学方法具有全面的性能优势。

Abstract: Weak instruments are a well known limitation for valid causal inference in Mendelian randomization studies. In the single exposure setting, weak instrument bias can be mitigated by selecting genetic instruments which are strongly associated with the exposure according to p-value and/or F-statistic thresholds. However, in the multi-exposure setting, genetic instruments may be strongly associated with an exposure but weakly associated with it conditional on all other exposures in the analysis. It is therefore more difficult to guarantee conditionally strong instruments in multivariable Mendelian randomization. Weak instrument bias can be mitigated using modelling approaches, however there are fewer methods for doing this in the multivariable case compared with the single exposure case. In this paper, we consider a method for mitigating weak instrument bias in multivariable Mendelian randomization using a Bayesian framework: MVMR-Pony. We compare this method with existing frequentist methods. We show using simulation studies that the MVMR-Pony method outperforms the frequentist approaches with respect to bias, coverage, type I error rates, and power, across settings where weak instrument bias arises due to correlated genetic effects, measurement error, and mediation.

</details>


### [269] [End-to-end probabilistic hierarchical forecasting of large hierarchies via probabilistic top-down](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26774)
*Lorenzo Zambon,Dario Azzimonti,Giorgio Corani*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: e2eTD 是一种快速可扩展的概率分层时序预测方法，仅需直接预测少量顶层聚合序列（约0.3%），再通过新颖的概率自顶向下采样算法将预测传播至底层，从而生成所有层级一致的概率预测。在 M5 和 Favorita 两个最大公开零售数据集上取得最优加权缩放分位数损失，运行时间仅需5-20分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售与供应链决策需要既概率化又跨层级一致的需求预测，但现有两步法（先预测后协调）和端到端神经模型在大规模分层时序（数千条）上面临计算成本过高、依赖强假设或需专用硬件等问题，无法在实际运营中高效落地。

Method: 提出 e2eTD 方法：(1) 仅直接预测少量顶层聚合序列（占层级约0.3%），这些序列更平滑、更易预测；(2) 将历史分解比例建模为联合分布，在样本内进行估计；(3) 通过新颖的概率自顶向下采样算法，将顶层预测样本传播至底层；(4) 对底层联合样本求和，获得所有聚合层级的一致概率预测。

Result: 在 M5 和 Favorita 数据集上，e2eTD 在所有聚合层级上取得了所有对比方法中最低的加权缩放分位数损失（WRMSSE），在 M5 Uncertainty 竞赛中可排第11名（共892支队伍）。在普通笔记本电脑上，M5（约4万条序列）运行约5分钟，Favorita（约30万条序列）运行约20分钟。

Conclusion: e2eTD 通过聚焦顶层预测与概率自顶向下传播，实现了大规模分层时序的高效概率一致预测，在精度和计算效率上均显著优于现有方法，为零售级运营决策提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Retail and supply chain operations rely on demand forecasts to drive decisions, from replenishment at the product level to capacity planning at the store level. These forecasts should be probabilistic, to allow risk-aware decisions, and coherent across the aggregation hierarchy, so that decisions taken at different levels are not based on conflicting demand forecasts. However, producing coherent probabilistic forecasts is computationally demanding; at retail scale, with hierarchies of thousands of time series, this cost becomes a first-order operational concern. Existing two-step forecast-then-reconcile procedures and end-to-end neural models scale poorly, rely on restrictive assumptions, or require specialized hardware and engineering effort. We propose e2eTD, a fast and scalable method for probabilistic coherent forecasting of large hierarchical and grouped time series. e2eTD directly forecasts only a small subset of aggregate series (about 0.3\% of the hierarchy in our experiments), which are smoother and thus more predictable than the intermittent bottom series. The resulting forecast samples are propagated to the bottom level through a novel probabilistic top-down sampling algorithm, in which the historical disaggregation proportions are modeled as joint distributions, estimated in-sample. Coherent forecasts for all aggregation levels are then obtained by summing the joint bottom-level samples. On the two largest publicly available retail datasets, M5 and Favorita, e2eTD achieves the lowest weighted scaled pinball loss (the M5 competition's probabilistic score) across aggregation levels among all competing methods; it would have ranked 11th of 892 teams in the M5 Uncertainty competition. On a standard laptop, e2eTD runs in about five minutes on M5 ($\sim$40K series) and twenty minutes on Favorita ($\sim$300K series).

</details>


### [270] [Multiple testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26781)
*Jesse Hemerik*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于多重假设检验的综述性教材，涵盖多种误差准则、检验流程及相应的R软件包，曾用作博士课程讲义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为博士生提供多重假设检验的系统性入门知识，填补该领域教学材料的空白。

Method: 以综述形式系统介绍多重检验中的各类误差控制准则（如FWER、FDR等）和相应的统计检验方法，并辅以R语言实现。

Result: 形成了一套完整的教学讲义，覆盖多重假设检验的核心概念、方法和实践工具。

Conclusion: 该讲义为多重假设检验的学习和教学提供了结构化的参考材料，兼具理论深度与实践指导价值。

Abstract: This text provides an introduction to multiple hypothesis testing. It covers various error criteria and testing procedures, and includes references to relevant R packages. An earlier version of this text served as the lecture notes for a PhD-level course on multiple testing.

</details>


### [271] [Structured Secant Methods to Select Smoothing Parameters For General Smooth Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26804)
*Joshua Krause,Jelmer P. Borst,Jacolien van Rij*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种准牛顿变体的扩展Fellner-Schall (qEFS)方法，通过结构化有限记忆割线近似替代对数似然的Hessian矩阵，避免高阶导数推导与计算，用于高效优化平滑参数。该方法在特定条件下收敛于EFS方法，并在仿真和实际模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义平滑模型中，优化平滑参数需优化拉普拉斯近似贝叶斯边缘似然。现有二阶方法需Hessian矩阵（精确优化甚至需四阶导数），推导困难且计算昂贵。亟需一种更易实现、计算成本更低的优化方法。

Method: 提出qEFS方法：采用结构化有限记忆割线近似代替完整的对数似然Hessian，本质为一阶方法；可选择性地累积近似某个子块，同时约束其余列与真实Hessian一致，利用精确列提供的结构提升子块近似精度。

Result: qEFS在特定条件下收敛于EFS方法，且在这些条件之外仍能提供良好估计。仿真研究验证了其性能。在涉及Hessian的次级任务（置信区间覆盖与模型选择）中，部分近似可达到接近名义水平的性能。在隐马尔可夫模型和Tweedie模型示例中，qEFS比替代方法显著更易实现。

Conclusion: qEFS方法为广义平滑模型中的平滑参数优化提供了一种实用、高效且易于实现的一阶替代方案，在保持良好统计性能的同时大幅降低了计算和推导负担。

Abstract: General smooth models replace parameters of a regular likelihood with additive models. The models can include parametric terms, Gaussian random effects, and smooth functions of covariates. The latter are parameterized via a reduced-rank spline basis and regularized via weighted quadratic penalties placed on the basis coefficients. Estimates for these weights (i.e., smoothing parameters) can be obtained by optimizing the Laplace-approximate Bayesian marginal likelihood. Existing (second-order) methods require the Hessian of the log-likelihood to solve this optimization problem approximately - exact optimization requires up to fourth order derivatives - which can be difficult to derive and expensive to evaluate. To address these problems, we present a quasi-Newton variant of the second-order Extended Fellner-Schall (EFS) optimization method. Our qEFS method relies on structured limited-memory secant approximations to the Hessian of the log-likelihood and is principally first-order. However, the approximation can also be accumulated for a sub-block of the Hessian, with the remaining columns being constrained to match those of the actual Hessian. The exact columns then provide additional structure for the sub-block approximation, which becomes more accurate as a result. We show that the qEFS method converges to the EFS method under certain conditions and continues to provide good estimates beyond these circumstances, which we illustrate in simulation studies. Secondary tasks involving the Hessian (confidence interval coverage & model selection) require partial approximations to achieve close to nominal performance. We provide Hidden Markov and Tweedie model examples, for which the qEFS method is substantially easier to implement than alternative methods.

</details>


### [272] [Optimizing Experimental Design for Causal Effect Estimation with Partial Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26818)
*Leopold Mareis*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了在给定预算约束下，如何通过最优分配完整样本和部分样本（如仅观测工具变量与处理变量）来最小化工具变量回归中因果效应估计的渐近方差，并在高斯图模型框架下给出了解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工具变量回归中，获取完整的三元组样本（X₁, X₂, X₃）可能成本高昂，而仅观测部分变量（如X₁₂）成本较低。作者希望利用先验分布信息，在有限预算下通过最优采样策略（混合完整样本与部分样本）提高因果效应估计的精度。

Method: 在高斯图模型框架下，推导一致估计量的渐近方差表达式；建立预算约束优化问题，求解实数域上的解析最优解，确定完整样本与部分样本的最优数量；基于最优分配进行显著性水平、检验功效和样本量计算。

Result: 该方法能够解析地求出最优采样方案，在保持估计一致性的同时显著降低所需预算和完整样本数量，并在汽车分析和药物研究两个实际场景中验证了其优势。

Conclusion: 在特定参数配置下，通过自适应地混合采集部分样本和完整样本，可以在给定预算约束下显著提升工具变量因果效应估计的效率，具有广泛的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Instrumental variable regression quantifies causal effects between a possibly confounded treatment variable $ X_2 $ and a response variable $ X_3 $ by leveraging an instrument $ X_1 $. Our work considers the setting where some prior information of the joint distribution of $ X_{123} $ is given, potentially through an initial dataset. However, further samples must be gathered to improve the accuracy of the estimation. We show that under specific parameter configurations in a Gaussian graphical model, taking partial samples from, e.g., $ X_{12} $ can reduce the asymptotic variance of a consistent estimator. This idea is developed by adding a budget constraint over the cost per (partial) sample. The optimization problem is analytically solvable over the real numbers and gives the optimal number of requested partial and complete samples. We provide significance level, power, and sample-size calculations for detecting a non-zero causal effect under optimal budget allocation. Our method can considerably reduce the necessary budget and the number of complete samples. Finally, we showcase the advantages and applicability of adaptive causal effect estimation for automotive analytics and pharmaceutical research.

</details>


### [273] [High-dimensional reliability-oriented Shapley effect estimation with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26826)
*Monteiro Lucas,Morio Jérôme,Demange-Chryst Julien,Bachoc François*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在存在大量相关输入变量时，利用单一失效样本点集估计面向可靠性的Shapley效应的新方案。该方法通过Normalizing Flows估计失效条件下的高维边际密度，并提供了基于同一样本的误差估计程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可靠性分析中，当模型包含大量相关输入变量时，传统的面向可靠性的Shapley效应估计面临高维条件密度估计困难、计算成本高的问题，亟需一种仅利用单一失效样本点的高效估计方法。

Method: 首先，将面向可靠性的闭合Sobol指标重新表述为涉及失效条件下边际密度的形式；接着，利用Normalizing Flows（一种生成建模工具）从可用失效样本中估计这些可能为高维的条件密度；此外，提出了一种基于同一样本的误差估计程序。

Result: 在数值用例上验证了所提方法的有效性，证明了该方法能够利用单一失效样本集准确估计面向可靠性的Shapley效应，并能评估估计误差。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了高维相关输入下可靠性Shapley效应估计的难题，Normalizing Flows在估计高维条件密度方面表现出色，基于同一样本的误差估计是重要贡献，未来可进一步拓展应用。

Abstract: This article presents a new estimation scheme for the reliability-oriented Shapley effects when there is a large number of correlated input variables in the model, using a unique sample of failure points. To do so, we first propose a new writing of the reliability-oriented closed Sobol indices involving the marginal densities conditionally to the failure, which may be high-dimensional. Then, we propose to estimate these densities with the available failing samples using Normalizing Flows, powerful tools from generative modeling that enable the estimation of complex high-dimensional densities. In addition, we provide an error estimation procedure relying on the same sample of failing points, which constitutes a new contribution for the estimation of target Shapley effects. Finally, we illustrate our methodology on numerical use-cases, discuss insightful features of our approach and provide prospects for the future.

</details>


### [274] [Semiparametric Inference for Half-Trek Estimators in Linear Structural Equation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26931)
*Leopold Mareis,Nils Sturma,Mathias Drton*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文推导了半参数影响函数，为HTC识别条件下的线性结构方程模型给出了渐近正态性证明与闭式方差公式，从而为HTC估计量构建了置信区间和Wald检验，并在Fulton鱼市数据上进行了实证应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半迹准则（HTC）为含隐混杂变量的有向混合图中结构系数的可识别性提供了图准则和闭式估计量，但该估计量的渐近分布尚未被推导，导致无法构建有效的标准误和置信区间。

Method: 在HTC识别的有向混合图（含环图）上，推导HTC估计量的半参数影响函数；通过将目标节点的结构残差与识别工具变量结合，并逐阶段递归修正前期估计带来的不确定性，进而证明估计量渐近正态，并给出闭式方差表达式。

Result: 得到了HTC估计量的影响函数，证明了其渐近正态性，方差可用闭式计算；据此可构造边际置信区间和Wald检验。在Fulton鱼市数据上，完整推断出供给对需求的因果效应。

Conclusion: HTC估计量具备良好的大样本性质，影响函数框架下的方差估计为含隐混杂的线性SEM提供了完整的推断工具，可推广至含环图情形。

Abstract: Linear structural equation models on directed mixed graphs encode causal relationships among variables subject to latent confounding. The half-trek criterion (HTC) provides a graphical sufficient condition for the structural coefficients to be rationally identifiable from the observable covariance matrix, and yields a corresponding closed-form rational estimator. Despite this, the asymptotic distribution of the HTC estimator, and hence valid standard errors and confidence regions, have not been derived. We derive the semiparametric influence function of this estimator for all HTC-identified directed mixed graphs, including cyclic ones. The influence function combines the structural residual at the target node with the identification instruments, recursively corrected for uncertainty from earlier estimation stages. The HTC estimator is asymptotically normal with variance computable in closed form, yielding confidence regions, marginal intervals, and Wald tests for individual structural coefficients. Applied to the Fulton Fish Market dataset, our theory delivers a complete inferential summary for the causal effect of supply on demand.

</details>


### [275] [Exact Comparison of Explanatory Strength of Two Dependent Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26949)
*Tomáš Mrkvička,Jan Radimský*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的非参数检验方法——配对交换置换检验（Paired Swap Permutation Test），用于比较两个相依预测变量对同一目标变量的相对解释力。该方法通过对称的组内交换机制和ECDF映射步骤，在保持边缘分布和协方差结构的同时实现精确推断，有效克服了传统方法在重尾分布和类别稀疏条件下的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较两个相依预测变量对共同目标变量的相对解释力是各学科中的基础问题。然而，经典渐近方法（如Vuong检验、Hotelling-Williams检验）在重尾分布和极端类别稀疏等病理数据条件下常常失效。朴素置换检验破坏了相依预测变量间的协方差结构，而对偶自助法在备择假设下评估方差时会引入人工平局、度量空间压缩和类别遗漏等问题，在有限样本中高度不可靠。因此，亟需一种能够在保持相依结构的同时提供精确推断的非参数方法。

Method: 提出"配对交换置换检验"（Paired Swap Permutation Test），核心原理基于零假设下的函数可交换性。对于分类数据，采用对称的组内交换机制；对于连续数据，引入经验累积分布函数（ECDF）映射步骤。这种基于Copula的转置方法能够完美保留边缘密度和经验支撑，且不引入重采样平局。

Result: 通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟，证明了所提检验在标准方法变得灾难性宽松或病态保守的条件下，能够严格维持名义显著性水平并最大化统计功效。在意大利语名词-名词复合词的高维语言数据集上的应用进一步验证了该方法在传统分析方法固有问题环境下的稳健精确推断能力。

Conclusion: 配对交换置换检验是一种精确的非参数方法，能够在保持相依预测变量协方差结构的同时进行可靠推断，有效解决了传统渐近检验和重采样方法在复杂数据条件下的根本性缺陷，在理论和应用层面均具有重要价值。

Abstract: Comparing the relative explanatory power of two dependent predictors regarding a common target variable is a fundamental challenge across scientific disciplines. Classical asymptotic procedures, such as Vuong's closeness test or the Hotelling-Williams test, frequently collapse under pathological data conditions, including heavy-tailed distributions and extreme categorical sparsity. To bypass these limitations, practitioners often turn to non-parametric resampling. However, naive permutation tests destroy the natural covariance structure of dependent predictors, while the paired bootstrap evaluating variance around the alternative hypothesis and introducing artificial ties suffers from metric space compression and categorical omission, rendering it highly unreliable in finite samples.
  In this paper, we introduce the Paired Swap Permutation Test, a novel and exact non-parametric methodology. Grounded in the principle of functional exchangeability under the null hypothesis, our algorithm utilizes a symmetric within-subject swapping mechanism for categorical data, and introduces an Empirical Cumulative Distribution Function (ECDF) mapping step for continuous domains. This copula-based transposition perfectly preserves marginal densities and the empirical support without introducing resampling ties.
  Through extensive Monte Carlo simulations, we demonstrate that the proposed test strictly maintains the nominal significance level and maximizes statistical power under conditions where standard methods become catastrophically liberal or pathologically conservative. Finally, we apply the framework to a high-dimensional linguistic dataset of Italian noun-noun compounds, proving its capacity to deliver robust, exact inference in environments where conventional analytical methods inherently fail.

</details>


### [276] [Predicting disease severity and large-scale spread from coupled severity measurements and imperfect indicators: Application to beet yellows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26972)
*Baptiste Oger,César Martinez,François Joudelat,Samuel Soubeyrand,Lionel Benoit*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段统计框架，利用具有广域时空覆盖的间接指标（如卫星遥感数据）来预测局部尺度的疾病严重程度，并重建大尺度疾病动态。方法结合了堆叠栅栏模型（stacked hurdle model）与随机森林处理零膨胀数据，以及半参数时空模型进行大尺度重建，以法国甜菜黄化病监测为案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疾病管理中，直接测量数据（如实地采样）虽然精确但稀疏且成本高昂，而间接指标（如遥感数据）虽含噪声但覆盖广、成本低。需要一种方法能有效融合这两类数据，既能在局部尺度预测疾病严重程度，又能在大尺度重建时空流行病动态。

Method: 提出一个两阶段统计框架。第一阶段：构建基于多重随机森林的堆叠栅栏模型（stacked hurdle model），从间接指标中预测局部疾病严重程度，能够处理疾病数据的零膨胀特性（大量零值）和时空结构。第二阶段：使用半参数时空模型，将第一阶段基于指标的预测结果进行时空平滑，重建大尺度的流行病动态。方法设计注重通用性和模块化。

Result: 以2019至2023年法国甜菜黄化病监测为案例，结合稀疏的田间实地测量与卫星遥感数据，验证了该方法的有效性，展示了如何利用间接指标补充直接测量，实现从局部预测到大尺度动态重建的完整分析链条。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段统计框架能够有效整合稀疏直接测量与噪声间接指标，在植物健康尤其是甜菜黄化病监测中表现良好。该方法具有通用性和模块化优势，可推广至人类和动物健康的疾病动态分析中，为基于多源数据的疾病时空监测提供了新工具。
